### [1] [An Open-Source Framework to Emulate Delay and Disruption Tolerant Networks for International Space Station Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21624)
*Krit Grover,Marcelo Ponce*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个开源的、完整的安全DTN Bundle协议栈实现，用于与国际空间站通信，包含身份验证、加密、分片、队列管理和 custody transfer 等功能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在挑战性网络环境（特别是空间系统）中，端到端连接无法保证，DTN对可靠通信至关重要。现有缺乏开源、完整的Bundle协议实现，需要提供一个功能完备、安全且可用于教育的系统。

Method: 实现了完整的Bundle协议栈，包括：1）安全特性：Bundle Authentication Block (BAB)、Payload Integrity Block (PIB)、Payload Confidentiality Block (PCB)，使用HMAC-SHA256和AES-256-CBC加密；2）核心功能：bundle分片与重组、优先级队列、custody transfer机制（ACK/NAK）、自动重传；3）现代响应式Web前端界面。

Result: 成功开发了一个开源、全栈的Bundle协议实现，具备完整的安全特性和核心功能，可用于与国际空间站的通信，并提供了交互式Web界面。

Conclusion: 该工作在计算机网络领域具有重要意义：提供了关键可靠协议的开源实现，填补了空白；为计算机网络和通信领域提供了交互式教育和学习框架。

Abstract: Delay and Disruption Tolerant Networks (DTN) are critical for reliable communications in challenged network environments, particularly for space systems where end-to-end connectivity cannot be guaranteed. We present an open-source, full-stack implementation of the Bundle protocol for communicating with the International Space Station (ISS), with complete security features including Bundle Authentication Block (BAB), Payload Integrity Block (PIB), and Payload Confidentiality Block (PCB) using HMAC-SHA256 and AES-256-CBC encryption. The system includes bundle fragmentation and reassembly, priority-based queuing, custody transfer with ACK/NAK mechanisms, and automatic retransmission. Our system also includes a frontend facilitated by a modern responsive web interface. We consider this work highly relevant in the context of computer networking because: i) it demonstrates a full stack, open-source, freely available implementation of this critical and reliable protocol; and ii) it offers an interactive educational and learning framework in the field of computer networks and communications.

</details>


### [2] [Lost in the Prefix: Revisiting IP Geolocation Accuracy Across Networks and Geographies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21937)
*Syed Tauhidun Nabi,Jocelyn Bliton,Tijay Chung,Shaddi Hasan*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 对四大IP地理定位数据库的大规模评估发现，移动网络误差比固定网络高10倍以上，全球南方地区失败率显著高于北方，根本原因是前缀粒度过粗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IP地理定位数据库广泛应用于研究、政策和工业领域，但其在不同网络类型和地理区域的准确性特征尚不明确，亟需系统性评估以揭示其局限性。

Method: 基于RIPE Atlas和UNICEF Giga的真实数据，对MaxMind GeoLite2、IPinfo、IP2Location和DB-IP四大主流提供商进行大规模评估，覆盖175个国家。

Result: 移动网络中位误差为179-207公里，远超固定网络的3-16公里；亚洲失败率53-61%，非洲66-72%，而欧洲仅9-20%；移动网络和全球南方地区的前缀粒度更粗，约70%的移动前缀跨越超过100公里。

Conclusion: 前缀粒度是导致IP地理定位误差的核心结构性因素，前缀越粗糙则误差越大，这一规律在不同提供商、网络类型和地理区域中均成立。

Abstract: IP geolocation databases are widely used in research, policy, and industry, yet their accuracy across network types and geographies remains poorly characterized. We present a large scale evaluation of four major providers (MaxMind GeoLite2, IPinfo, IP2Location, and DB-IP) using ground truth from RIPE Atlas and UNICEF Giga across 175 countries. We find that mobile networks exhibit median errors more than 10 times higher than fixed networks across all providers (179--207~km vs.\ 3--16~km), and that Global South regions show significantly higher failure rates than Global North: Asia exceeds 53--61\% and Africa 66--72\%, compared to 9--20\% in Europe. We trace both gaps to a shared structural source: provider prefixes in mobile networks and Global South geographies are more likely to be coarser than BGP announcements, and approximately 70\% of mobile prefixes span more than 100~km geographically. Our findings point to prefix granularity as a common explanatory factor: coarser prefixes consistently produce the highest errors regardless of provider, network type, or geography.

</details>


### [3] [Toward Realistic Wi-Fi Fault Diagnosis: A Multi-Modal Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22008)
*Junjian Zhang,Haobo Deng,Xinxin Li,Ming Zhao,Fengxiao Tang,Nei Kato*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文构建了首个公开的多模态Wi-Fi故障诊断数据集，包含10,000+故障样本，并建立了统一基准测试。实验发现现有诊断方法在利用异构运维数据方面仍存在挑战，LLM方法的评估框架揭示了未来研究的重要方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代网络智能运维系统持续产生大量多模态运维数据，但在异构运维环境下的Wi-Fi故障诊断研究仍然不足，缺乏公开的跨层异构运维数据集和统一基准。

Method: 1) 构建部署在校园工作环境的真实Wi-Fi测试平台，配备自动故障注入系统；2) 收集包含10,000+故障样本的多模态Wi-Fi故障数据集（首个公开捕获异构跨层运维观测的数据集）；3) 建立跨越多诊断任务、运维模态和代表性诊断范式的统一基准；4) 开发面向推理的评估框架评估LLM诊断方法。

Result: 实验结果表明：1) 现有诊断方法在有效利用异构运维数据方面仍面临挑战；2) LLM方法的诊断分析与实际网络状况之间的一致性评估揭示了关键问题；3) 为未来多模态Wi-Fi诊断研究提供了重要参考。

Conclusion: 本研究为Wi-Fi故障诊断领域提供了首个公开的多模态跨层数据集和统一基准，揭示了现有方法在处理异构运维数据时的局限性，并为未来基于LLM的多模态Wi-Fi诊断研究指明了重要方向。

Abstract: Intelligent network operation and maintenance systems in modern networks continuously generate large volumes of multi-modal operational data. However, Wi-Fi fault diagnosis under heterogeneous operational environments remains insufficiently understood. We build a real-world Wi-Fi testbed deployed in campus working environments with an automated fault injection system, and collect a multi-modal Wi-Fi fault dataset containing over 10,000 fault samples across diverse wireless scenarios. To the best of our knowledge, this is among the first publicly available datasets jointly capturing heterogeneous cross-layer operational observations for Wi-Fi fault diagnosis. Based on this dataset, we establish a unified benchmark spanning multiple diagnosis tasks, operational modalities, and representative diagnosis paradigms. Experimental results indicate that effectively leveraging heterogeneous operational data remains challenging for existing diagnosis approaches. We further evaluate emerging LLM-based approaches and develop a reasoningoriented evaluation framework to assess the consistency between generated diagnostic analyses and actual network conditions. Our findings suggest several important considerations for future multi-modal Wi-Fi diagnosis.

</details>


### [4] [Astragalus: Automatic Configuration Repair for Production Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22092)
*Zhenrong Gu,Peng Zhang,Xing Feng,Xu Liu*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Astragalus，一种基于语法驱动的网络配置自动修复方法，通过"定位-修复-验证"迭代流程，在合成网络中修复100%的故障，在真实网络中修复97.5%的故障，平均耗时7.36秒，在生产网络中6分钟内提供有效修复方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络配置容易出错并导致灾难性服务中断，现有语义驱动的自动配置修复工具需要将网络语义建模为SMT约束进行求解，由于网络语义复杂，构建和求解这些约束代价高昂，导致工具缺乏通用性和可扩展性。受自动程序修复启发，作者探索语法驱动的方法来避免显式建模语义。

Method: 提出Astragalus方法，采用语法驱动方法进行自动配置修复，通过多次迭代"定位-修复-验证"流程搜索修复方案，通过"嫁接"仓库中已有代码进行修复，无需建模程序语义。

Result: 在注入15种类型错误的多个规模合成网络中修复了所有故障，在真实网络中修复了97.5%的故障，平均耗时7.36秒；在拥有O(1,000)Õ(10,000)设备的真实生产网络中，针对4个近期网络事件或非预期变更，在6分钟内提供了有效修复方案。

Conclusion: 语法驱动的自动配置修复方法可以避免语义驱动方法的高昂代价，在保持高效的同时具有良好的通用性和可扩展性，为运营商提供了一种实用的自动配置修复工具。

Abstract: Network configurations are prone to errors, which can lead to catastrophic service outages. A tool that can achieve automatic configuration repair (ACR) is highly desired by operators. Existing tools for ACR follow a semantic-driven approach: they model network semantics as a set of SMT constraints, and solve them for a location or fix of the error. Due to the complex semantics of networks, constructing and solving these constraints can be prohibitively expensive, making these tools neither general nor scalable. Inspired by automatic program repair (APR), we explore another direction, i.e., a syntax-driven approach, which tries to repair program bugs by ``grafting'' some existing code in the same repository, without modeling program semantics. Following this direction, we propose Astragalus, a syntax-driven method for ACR. It uses multiple iterations of a ``localize-fix-validate'' pipeline to search for repairs, and proves quite effective on configurations of our production network. Specifically, we show that Astragalus can repair every incident in multiple sizes of a synthesized network, and 97.5\% of the incidents on a real network, both with 15 types of errors injected, within an average time of 7.36 seconds. It has also provided valid repair options in under 6 minutes for 4 recent network incidents or undesired changes, in a real production network with O(1,000)Õ(10,000) devices.

</details>


### [5] [High-speed Networking for Giga-Scale AI Factories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21187)
*Sajy Khashab,Albert Gran Alcoz,Alon Gal,Jacky Romano,Rani Abboud,Yonatan Piasetzky,Lior Maman,Amit Nishry,Barak Gafni,Omer Shabtai,Matty Kadosh,Dror Goldenberg,Gilad Shainer,Mark Silberstein*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了NVIDIA Spectrum-X多平面架构，通过拓扑并行性和硬件加速负载均衡，实现了大规模分布式AI训练网络的高性能、低延迟和强隔离性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着分布式模型训练扩展到数十万GPU规模，横向扩展网络面临前所未有的性能和效率挑战，需要可预测、稳定的网络性能，同时满足高利用率和低延迟的需求。

Method: 提出Spectrum-X多平面架构，采用拓扑并行性替代层级深度，并在网卡和交换机中引入硬件加速负载均衡，以在微秒时间尺度上快速响应高度动态的网络条件。

Result: 在生产级AI基础设施上实现了理论线路速率的98%，低抖动无延迟；强跨租户隔离；健壮的、与容量成比例的双分带宽，10%链路故障仅导致7%延迟增加；能快速响应LLM训练期间的主机和链路波动。

Conclusion: Spectrum-X架构通过创新的多平面设计和硬件加速机制，成功解决了大规模AI训练网络的性能、隔离性和鲁棒性挑战，为构建高性能AI基础设施提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: As distributed model training scales to span hundreds of thousands of GPUs, scale-out networks face unprecedented performance and efficiency demands. NVIDIA Spectrum-X Ethernet has been designed from the ground up to achieve predictable and stable network performance with high utilization and low latency. This paper presents the Spectrum-X multiplane architecture, which replaces hierarchical depth with topological parallelism, and introduces hardware-accelerated load balancing in NICs and switches as the key architectural approach to provide fast reaction to highly dynamic network conditions at the microsecond timescales that AI training workloads demand. We describe the motivation, design principles, evaluation methodology and performance on state-of-the-art benchmarks, as well as the lessons we learned from deploying and debugging Spectrum-X networks in large-scale systems. Our evaluation highlights production-grade AI infrastructure performance across three core dimensions: 98% of the theoretical line rate with low jitter-free latency; strong cross-tenant isolation for concurrent workloads; robust, capacity-proportional bisection bandwidth and 7% latency increase for 10% fabric link failures; and rapid reaction to host and fabric link flaps during LLM training workloads.

</details>


### [6] [Latency in Real-Time 3D Volumetric Streaming: A Comprehensive Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22131)
*Seungwoo Hong,Hosun Yoon,Seong Moon,Inayat Ali*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文对实时3D体积流媒体系统进行了全面的延迟测量与分析，通过分解应用层、传输协议层和网络层三个关键层次，识别延迟瓶颈并提出针对性优化策略，以提升系统响应性和用户体验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时3D体积流媒体技术在VR、AR、游戏、远程协作等领域具有重要应用价值，但延迟问题严重影响用户沉浸感、引发晕动症并破坏实时交互体验。解决延迟问题对于提升用户体验和系统效率至关重要，因此需要系统性地分析和优化流媒体系统中的延迟。

Method: 将实时体积流媒体过程系统性地分解为三个关键层次：应用层、传输协议层和网络层。在真实世界系统中对每一层进行评估，识别延迟瓶颈，量化其影响，并揭示延迟的根本原因。基于分析结果提出针对性的优化策略。

Result: 成功识别了实时体积流媒体系统中各层次的延迟瓶颈，量化了延迟影响，发现了延迟的根本原因。提出了针对性的优化策略来减少延迟并提升系统响应性，建立了提高实时3D体积流媒体效率、可扩展性和用户体验的最佳实践。

Conclusion: 本研究通过系统性的延迟分析和优化策略，为实时3D体积流媒体领域建立了最佳实践和创新解决方案，推动了该领域的发展，为实现更沉浸、更响应的数字环境铺平了道路。

Abstract: Real-time 3D volumetric streaming is a transformative technology that enables the seamless transmission and rendering of high-fidelity 3D models, enhancing applications in virtual reality (VR), augmented reality (AR), gaming, telepresence, and remote collaboration. However, latency remains a major challenge, affecting immersion, causing motion sickness, and disrupting real-time interactions. Addressing these latency issues is essential for improving user experience and ensuring system efficiency. This study conducts a comprehensive latency measurement and analysis within a real-time volumetric streaming environment. We systematically break down the streaming process into three key layers: the application layer, the transport protocol layer, and the network layer. By evaluating each layer in a real-world system, we identify latency bottlenecks, quantify their impact, and uncover the underlying causes of delay. Based on these findings, we propose targeted optimization strategies to mitigate latency and enhance system responsiveness. Through this research, we establish best practices and innovative solutions to improve the efficiency, scalability, and overall user experience of real-time 3D volumetric streaming. Our insights contribute to advancing the field, paving the way for more immersive and responsive digital environments.

</details>


### [7] [Impact of Atmospheric Turbulence and Pointing Error on Earth Observation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22268)
*Celia Sánchez-de-Miguel,Antonio M. Mercado-Martínez,Beatriz Soret,Antonio Jurado-Navas,Miguel Castillo-Vázquez*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种增强型图像模拟器，用于生成包含大气湍流和卫星指向抖动的物理真实退化图像，研究表明现有AI检测模型在退化条件下性能显著下降，强调了在训练数据中纳入真实物理退化的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地球观测图像常受大气湍流和指向抖动影响而退化，但现有AI检测模型的训练数据集很少考虑这些效应，这可能导致模型在实际操作环境中的性能不可靠。

Method: 开发了一种增强型图像模拟器，能够引入垂直路径大气湍流和由平台及传感器振动引起的卫星指向抖动，生成物理真实的退化图像。以船舶检测为案例研究，使用YOLOv8和RetinaNet在不同湍流和指向误差水平下评估检测性能。

Result: YOLOv8的召回率从理想条件下的91%降至弱湍流条件下的60%，在强湍流或抖动条件下降至40%以下；相比之下，RetinaNet表现出更强的鲁棒性，在退化条件下保持约75%的召回率。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，在地球观测训练数据集中纳入真实的物理退化对于确保AI模型在实际操作环境中的可靠性能至关重要，这在海上监视应用中得到了验证。

Abstract: Earth Observation (EO) imagery is often degraded by atmospheric turbulence and pointing jitter; yet, these effects are rarely considered in datasets used to train AI-based detection models. Based on prior work, this paper presents an enhanced image simulator that enables the incorporation of vertical-path atmospheric turbulence and satellite pointing jitter, arising from platform and sensor vibrations, to generate physically realistic distorted images. As a case study, vessel detection is evaluated using YOLOv8 and RetinaNet on images generated by the proposed simulator under different levels of turbulence and pointing errors. Results show that YOLOv8 recall decreases from 91% under ideal conditions to 60% in the presence of weak turbulence, and falls below 40% under strong turbulence or jitter. In contrast, RetinaNet demonstrates greater robustness, maintaining approximately 75% recall across degraded conditions. These results highlight the importance of incorporating realistic physical degradations into EO training datasets to ensure reliable performance of AI-based models in operational environments, as demonstrated in maritime surveillance applications.

</details>


### [8] [Throughput and Delay Performance of Slotted Aloha in SmartBANs under Saturation Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22317)
*Anastasios C. Politis,Constantinos S. Hilas*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文通过二维离散时间马尔可夫链模型分析了ETSI SmartBAN规范中时隙Aloha协议在饱和条件下的吞吐量和时延性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估欧洲电信标准协会(ETSI) SmartBAN规范定义的时隙Aloha协议在饱和条件下的性能表现，为协议性能预测和优化提供理论基础。

Method: 建立二维离散时间马尔可夫链(DTMC)模型来刻画协议运行细节，分析饱和吞吐量和平均端到端时延等性能指标，并通过仿真验证模型准确性。

Result: 理论分析与仿真结果高度吻合，验证了所提模型能够准确预测协议性能。

Conclusion: 该模型可用于SmartBAN时隙Aloha协议的性能预测和优化设计，为实际应用提供理论指导。

Abstract: This letter evaluates the performance of the slotted Aloha protocol defined by the European Telecommunication Standard Institute (ETSI) SmartBAN specification, under saturation conditions. For this purpose, we develop a two-dimensional Discrete Time Markov Chain (DTMC) to model the operational details of the protocol and assess its performance in terms of saturation throughput and average end-to-end delay. The accuracy of the proposed model is validated by means of simulation which reveals a very good match among theoretical and simulation results. The model can be used for protocol performance prediction and optimization purposes.

</details>


### [9] [Eliminating Premature Termination in Multihop Rendezvous for Cognitive Radio-based Emergency Response Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22325)
*Zahid Ali,Saritha Unnikrishnan,Eoghan Furey,Ian McLoughlin,Saim Ghafoor*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了MR-DMCA协议，通过解决多跳会合中的过早终止问题，实现了100%准确的邻居和拓扑发现，并将会合时间显著缩短。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在灾后环境中，受损的通信基础设施严重限制了应急响应团队之间的协调。认知无线电网络(CRNs)通过允许节点机会性地访问可用频谱来实现快速部署的通信。然而，现有的多跳会合协议通常依赖N-1终止条件，这可能导致过早终止，从而导致邻居发现不完整和网络拓扑形成无效。这一限制是多跳会合协议中以前被忽视的问题。

Method: 本文提出了一种多跳可靠双模块时钟算法(MR-DMCA)，该协议引入了坐标辅助的邻居验证机制和自主终止策略，确保在协议终止之前完成完整的邻居和拓扑发现，从而消除过早终止问题并确保可靠的网络形成。虽然该验证和终止方法在MR-DMCA中实现，但可应用于更广泛的多跳会合协议类别。

Result: 在具有20个节点和20个通道的高主无线电活动(m=2)的最坏情况下，MR-DMCA实现了100%准确的邻居和拓扑发现，同时与基线协议相比，会合时间分别减少了76%、37%和17%。

Conclusion: 解决过早终止问题对于基于认知无线电的应急通信网络中的可靠多跳会合至关重要，MR-DMCA协议成功实现了这一目标并展现了显著的性能提升。

Abstract: In post-disaster environments, damaged communication infrastructure severely limits coordination among emergency response teams. Cognitive radio networks (CRNs) enable rapidly deployable communication by allowing nodes to opportunistically access available spectrum. However, existing multihop rendezvous protocols typically rely on N-1 termination conditions, which can lead to premature termination, resulting in incomplete neighbour discovery and invalid network topology formation. This work identifies this limitation as a previously overlooked issue in multihop rendezvous protocols. This paper proposes a Multihop Reliable Dual-Modular Clock Algorithm (MR-DMCA) that eliminates premature termination and ensures reliable network formation. The proposed protocol introduces a coordinate-assisted neighbour validation mechanism and an autonomous termination strategy that guarantees complete neighbour and topology discovery before protocol termination. Although implemented within MR-DMCA, the proposed validation and termination approach is applicable to a wider class of multihop rendezvous protocols. Extensive simulations demonstrate that, in a worst-case scalable scenario with 20 nodes and 20 channels under high primary radio activity (m=2), MR-DMCA achieves 100% accurate neighbour and topology discovery while reducing rendezvous time by up to 76%, 37%, and 17% compared with baseline protocols. The results highlight that addressing premature termination is critical for reliable multihop rendezvous in cognitive radiobased emergency communication networks.

</details>


### [10] [EnCoR: An end-to-end architecture for simplifying cellular networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22524)
*Wesley Woo,Zhuowei Wen,Monniiesh Velmurugan,Richard Raad,Sylvia Ratnasamy,Scott Shenker,Shaddi Hasan*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出EnCoR架构，将移动性管理从蜂窝网络核心网中移除，采用端到端方式处理移动性。该方案在保持与现有认证、计费和QoS技术兼容的同时，将端到端延迟降低2.6倍，资本成本降低90%以上，且无需修改手机终端。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统蜂窝网络将移动性支持作为网络内服务，虽然为传统流量提供无缝连接，但导致终端用户延迟增加、网络复杂度和运营开销上升。然而现代应用和传输协议对移动性的容忍度日益增强，这促使作者重新审视"移动性必须作为网络内服务提供"这一传统假设。

Method: 提出EnCoR（端到端核心网和无线接入网）架构，完全从核心网中移除移动性管理功能。利用端到端移动性机制，消除基于隧道的IP锚定，同时保持与现有认证、计费和QoS技术的兼容性。该架构无需修改手机终端即可部署。

Result: 实验证明EnCoR在视频通话、语音通话和视频流等实际应用中性能与传统LTE网络相当。相比3GPP网络，EnCoR将端到端延迟降低，资本成本降低超过90%。在相同硬件约束下，核心网可处理更多切换事件，切换延迟降低2.6倍，整体切换控制信令量减少。

Conclusion: EnCoR证明了将移动性管理从核心网移至端到端的可行性，为蜂窝网络架构提供了新范式。该方案在性能、成本和扩展性方面均优于传统架构，为运营商提供了显著降低延迟和基础设施成本的有效途径，具有重要的实际部署价值。

Abstract: Since their creation, cellular networks have made in-network mobility support a key feature of their service model. While this approach provides seamless connectivity for legacy traffic, it has the side effects of inflating end-user latency and increasing complexity and operational overhead for operators. Yet modern applications and transport protocols are increasingly mobility tolerant, prompting us to revisit the assumption that mobility must be provided as an in-network service. In this paper, we propose EnCoR (End-to-End Core and RAN), a deployable cellular network architecture that removes mobility from the core entirely. Leveraging end-to-end mobility, EnCoR eliminates tunnel-based IP anchoring while preserving compatibility with existing authentication, charging, and QoS techniques. We demonstrate that EnCoR works with unmodified phones while providing equivalent performance as traditional LTE networks for real applications including video and voice calling and video streaming. We show that EnCoR not only allows network operators to reduce end to end latency, but can also reduce the capital cost of providing low latency service to users by more than 90% compared to 3GPP networks, based on cost estimates for cellular network core and border router infrastructure provided by the FCC. Finally, we demonstrate that these gains are achieved while reducing the amount of overall handover control messaging, allowing the EnCoR core network to handle a greater number of mobility handover events than an LTE core under identical hardware constraints, achieving a 2.6x lower handover latency under load.

</details>


### [11] [SCALE: Sensitivity-Aware Federated Unlearning with Information Freshness Optimization for Mobile Edge Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22589)
*Zihao Ding,Beining Wu,Jun Huang*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了SCALE，一个结合历史贡献分析和信息新鲜度自适应稀疏化的双层联邦遗忘框架，有效解决了现有方法遗忘精度低和缺乏时间信息反映的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的联邦遗忘方法存在遗忘精度低和缺乏时间信息反映的问题，导致遗忘性能欠佳，无法有效解决移动边缘计算系统中的数据污染和隐私合规问题。

Method: 提出SCALE双层遗忘框架：首先采用基于历史贡献的层敏感性分析来识别受目标客户端影响最大的层，然后在权重子组级别通过自适应稀疏化执行细粒度遗忘，以平衡信息新鲜度与遗忘效果。

Result: 理论分析证明了框架的收敛特性和加速优势；实验和测试平台结果表明，相比最先进的基线方法，该方法在遗忘效果方面具有显著优势，遗忘性能大幅提升。

Conclusion: SCALE框架通过双层结构有效结合历史贡献分析和信息新鲜度感知，成功解决了联邦遗忘中的关键问题，为移动边缘计算系统中的数据隐私保护提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Federated Unlearning (FU) is emerging as a powerful tool that enables the selective removal of client data to effectively address data contamination and meet strict privacy regulations in mobile edge computing (MEC) systems. Although FU has recently drawn attention in the AI community, existing approaches suffer from low unlearning precision and lack temporal information reflection, which results in suboptimal forgetting performance. To address these issues, we propose SCALE, a dual-level unlearning framework combining historical contribution analysis with information freshness-aware adaptive sparsification. Our framework first employs a historical contribution-based layer sensitivity analysis to identify layers most influenced by target clients, then performs fine-grained unlearning through adaptive sparsification at the weight sub-group level to balance information freshness with forgetting effectiveness. Through theoretical analysis, the proposed framework demonstrates the convergence properties and acceleration advantages. Our experiments and testbed results demonstrate superior unlearning effectiveness compared to state-of-the-art baselines, with significantly improved forgetting performance.

</details>


### [12] [An intensive vRAN deployment with OpenAirInterface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22638)
*Romain Beurdouche,Raymond Knopp*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文研究了在通用处理器和硬件加速器上使用OpenAirInterface 5G软件栈实现大规模vRAN部署的适配方法与性能表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 5G虚拟化无线接入网（vRAN）对计算架构提出了新挑战，需要在保证性能的前提下最大化网络的灵活性与效率，现有解决方案需进一步验证其在实际部署中的可行性与扩展性。

Method: 基于OpenAirInterface 5G软件栈，将其适配到多种计算架构（通用处理器与硬件加速器），优化软件栈以利用硬件能力，并在服务器上部署多个共享vRAN实例以测试扩展性。

Result: 提出了软件栈改进方案，量化了其对性能的提升效果，并观察到不同计算架构对vRAN部署行为的影响与性能差异。

Conclusion: 分析了当前vRAN部署的局限性，指出未来需进一步优化以实现更高效的vRAN部署，为计算架构选型与软件栈设计提供了实践参考。

Abstract: The advent of 5G virtualized Radio Access Networks (vRANs) brings a new challenge with regards to computer architectures. It requires to select or design computing technologies that provide a sufficient level of performance while maximizing the flexibility and efficiency of the implemented networks. Several solutions addressing this challenge were proposed, relying on general purpose processors as well as hardware accelerators. This work describes our effort to enable an intensive vRAN deployment using the 5G software stack OpenAirInterface on top of these computer architectures. We had to adapt the software stack to leverage the capabilities of hardware and to find how to scale up the vRAN deployment with several vRAN instances sharing a server. We describe in this work our improvements to the stack and their effect on performance. We also share our observations on the behavior of the computer architectures and how they affect our deployment. We finally discuss the limitations of our deployment and further efforts to implement better vRAN deployments.

</details>

### [13] [DynaFlow: Transparent and Flexible Intra-Device Parallelism via Programmable Operator Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21603)
*Yi Pan,Yile Gu,Jinbin Luo,Yibo Wu,Ziren Wang,Hongtao Zhang,Ziyi Xu,Shengkai Lin,Baris Kasikci,Stephanie Wang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出DynaFlow框架，通过解耦逻辑模型定义与物理执行调度，实现设备内并行策略的透明灵活集成，在6个SOTA ML系统中仅需最小代码改动即可获得最高1.29倍吞吐量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ML框架的静态顺序编程模型与设备内并行策略存在根本冲突，集成这些策略需要侵入式、模型特定的代码重构，工程成本高昂。且并行策略对执行上下文（工作负载、模型架构、硬件）高度敏感，迫使开发者实现和维护多个专用解决方案。

Method: DynaFlow框架解耦逻辑模型定义与物理执行调度：前端提供图划分注释和可编程接口用于定义自定义设备内并行策略；后端异步管理复杂控制/数据流，使用定制内存管理消除拷贝开销，并保持与CUDA Graphs和TorchInductor等优化的兼容性。

Result: DynaFlow能够以最小代码改动将代表性并行策略集成到6个SOTA ML系统中，实现最高1.29倍的吞吐量提升。

Conclusion: DynaFlow成功解决了设备内并行策略集成中的工程成本和可维护性问题，实现了透明、灵活且高效的集成方案，同时保持了与现有优化技术的兼容性。

Abstract: Intra-device parallelism addresses resource under-utilization in ML inference and training by overlapping the execution of operators with different resource usage. However, its wide adoption is hindered by a fundamental conflict with the static, sequential programming model of existing frameworks. Integrating these strategies requires invasive, model-specific code overhauls, representing an intractable engineering cost. This is further amplified by the high sensitivity of strategies to execution contexts (e.g., workload, model architecture, hardware), forcing developers to implement and maintain multiple specialized solutions. To address this, we propose DynaFlow, a framework that enables the transparent and flexible integration of intra-device parallelism by decoupling the logical model definition from the physical execution schedule. DynaFlow introduces a flexible frontend with annotations for graph partitioning and a programmable interface for defining custom intra-device parallelism strategies. Its efficient backend manages complex control/data-flow asynchronously, uses custom memory management to eliminate copy overheads, and preserves compatibility with optimizations like CUDA Graphs and TorchInductor. We demonstrate that DynaFlow can integrate representative parallelism strategies into 6 state-of-the-art ML systems with minimal code changes, achieving up to a 1.29x throughput improvement. DynaFlow is publicly available at https://github.com/uw-syfi/DynaFlow.

</details>


### [14] [LiveR: Fine-Grained Elasticity via Live Reconfiguration for Model Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22014)
*Haoyuan Liu,Kairui Zhou,Shuyao Qi,Qinwei Yang,Shengkai Lin,Shizhen Zhao,Wei Zhang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: LiveR是一种用于大模型训练的实时重配置运行时，通过内存直接传递替代传统的检查点重启机制，将弹性训练的重配置停机时间从分钟级降至秒级，加速比达14-23倍，在99%的训练吞吐下实现资源的高效利用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了降低成本并最大化集群利用率，大模型训练越来越多地使用竞价实例和可回收资源等易失性廉价GPU容量。然而，现有弹性训练系统将重配置视为停止-重启过程，需要通过检查点外部化分布式状态、重建运行时并重启训练，导致检查点I/O、进程重启、CUDA初始化和通信器设置等操作带来大量停机时间，无法有效应对这类环境提供的短暂预警窗口。

Method: LiveR提出了实时重配置运行时，用内存受限的实时传递替代基于存储的重启机制。其核心技术包括：(1)在当前训练世界继续训练时异步准备目标世界；(2)隔离启动新加入的工作节点，将重量级初始化移出关键路径；(3)通过高带宽互连直接流式传输模型状态，并在张量、流水线和数据并行维度上进行在线重塑；(4)执行轻量级提交操作切换到新配置，无需停止-重启。该系统基于Megatron-LM和PyTorch实现。

Result: 在多节点GPU集群上的端到端评估表明，LiveR在多样化重配置场景下将停机时间从分钟级降至秒级，相比检查点/重启基线实现14-23倍的重配置加速，产生的稳态开销极小，在易失性资源条件下能够维持高达99%的训练有效吞吐量。

Conclusion: LiveR成功解决了弹性大模型训练中的重配置效率瓶颈，通过创新的实时传递机制显著降低了重配置开销，使得竞价实例等易失性低成本GPU容量在大模型训练中变得切实可行，为大模型训练的成本优化提供了重要的技术支撑。

Abstract: To reduce user costs and maximize cluster utilization, large model training increasingly leverages volatile but inexpensive GPU capacity, such as spot instances and reclaimable resources in shared clusters. Yet, capitalizing on these economic benefits requires jobs to adapt within the short warning windows that many such environments provide. Existing elastic training systems still treat reconfiguration as stop-and-restart: they externalize distributed state through checkpoints, rebuild the distributed runtime on a new topology, and restart training, turning each resize event into a storage-heavy recovery procedure that incurs substantial downtime from checkpoint I/O, process restart, CUDA initialization, and communicator setup. We present LiveR, a live reconfiguration runtime for elastic LLM training that replaces storage-backed restart with a live, bounded-memory handoff between mixed-parallel training worlds. While the current world continues training, LiveR asynchronously prepares the target world, bootstraps newly added workers in isolation to keep heavyweight initialization off the critical path, and streams model state directly over high-bandwidth interconnects while reshaping it online across tensor, pipeline, and data parallel dimensions. Once the target world is ready, LiveR performs a lightweight commit that switches training to the new configuration without stop-and-restart on the live path. We implement LiveR atop Megatron-LM and PyTorch and evaluate it end-to-end on a multi-node GPU cluster. Across diverse reconfiguration scenarios, LiveR reduces downtime from minutes to seconds, accelerates reconfiguration by 14$\times$-23$\times$ over checkpoint/restart baselines, incurs minimal steady-state overhead, and sustains up to 99% training goodput under volatile-resource conditions, making volatile low-cost GPU capacity far more practical for LLM training.

</details>


### [15] [Secure and Parallel Determinant Computation for Large-Scale Matrices in Edge Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22039)
*Prajwal Panth*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了SPDC框架，通过轻量级加密（CED）和并行LU分解，在不可信边缘服务器上实现安全、高效的矩阵行列式计算，并提供两种结果完整性验证方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘计算环境下资源受限客户端需外包矩阵行列式计算（MDC）等密集任务，但传统算法三次复杂度过高且存在隐私泄露风险，难以满足实时性需求。

Method: 提出CED轻量级加密方案（结合EWO和PRT）保护矩阵隐私；利用并行LU分解将加密矩阵块分发至N个不可信边缘服务器；采用单向通信模型降低协调开销；设计Q_2（概率标量）和Q_3（确定性低复杂度）两种验证算法保障结果完整性。

Result: 数学分析表明该框架提供强隐私和安全保证、低计算开销及部署灵活性，适用于分布式边缘辅助系统的安全可扩展实时MDC。

Conclusion: SPDC框架有效解决了边缘计算环境下隐私保护与计算效率的矛盾，为资源受限设备提供了安全、可扩展的行列式计算解决方案。

Abstract: The advent of edge computing has enabled resource-constrained clients to delegate intensive computational tasks to distributed edge servers, especially within Internet of Things (IoT) environments. Among such tasks, Matrix Determinant Computation (MDC) remains critical for applications in control systems, cryptography, and machine learning. However, the cubic complexity of traditional determinant algorithms makes them unsuitable for real-time processing in constrained edge scenarios.
  We propose a Secure Parallel Determinant Computation (SPDC) framework, which provides strong security guaranties, including privacy-preserving MDC, across N distributed edge servers. The framework achieves privacy through Composite Element Distortion (CED) - a lightweight encryption method that combines Element-wise Obfuscation (EWO) and the Panth Rotation Theorem (PRT) to conceal both structural and numerical matrix content while preserving determinant properties. Parallel LU decomposition is used to distribute encrypted matrix blocks across an arbitrary number of untrusted edge servers, enabling efficient and scalable determinant computation. A one-way communication model further reduces coordination overhead by eliminating inter-server interactions. To ensure result integrity with minimal client burden, we further introduce two verification algorithms: Q_2, a probabilistic scalar method, and Q_3, a deterministic and low-complexity alternative.
  Mathematical analysis demonstrates that the proposed framework provides strong privacy and security guaranties, low computational overhead, and deployment flexibility - making it well-suited for secure, scalable, and real-time MDC in distributed edge-assisted systems.

</details>


### [16] [Nf-PEAK: Process-Based Energy Attribution for Nextflow Workflows on Kubernetes Clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22393)
*Philipp Thamm,Somayeh Mohammadi,Kathleen West,Knut Reinert,Lauritz Thamsen,Ulf Leser*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了Nf-PEAK，一种在共享Kubernetes集群中精确估算科学工作流任务级能耗的容器化方法，平均误差约6.6%-10.9%，优于现有工具Kepler。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学工作流在共享Kubernetes集群上通过Nextflow等引擎执行日益普遍，其能耗对成本和可持续性至关重要。由于工作流任务高度异构，需要单独分析和优化，但在集群环境中任务级能耗估算面临诸多挑战：Intel RAPL计数器仅报告节点级能耗、计数器和主机进程信息访问受限、并发负载引入资源竞争和测量噪声。

Method: Nf-PEAK采用四步法：(i)识别工作流Pod，(ii)通过cgroup元数据将Pod映射到主机进程，(iii)采样RAPL和每进程性能计数器，(iv)应用非线性能量分配模型后在任务级别聚合结果。该方法实现容器化，专门针对Nextflow工作流引擎设计。

Result: 在Kubernetes集群上评估三个nf-core工作流，在受控共存CPU负载下，Nf-PEAK在隔离运行中达到6.6%的平均平均绝对百分比误差(MAPE)，当不相关负载使每个节点32个硬件线程中的8个饱和时误差为10.9%，在2、3、4和8个节点间保持稳定。与最先进的Kubernetes工具Kepler相比，Nf-PEAK平均误差更低，特别是在共存负载下表现更优。

Conclusion: Nf-PEAK成功解决了在共享Kubernetes集群环境中精确估算科学工作流任务级能耗的难题，相比现有工具Kepler提供更准确的测量结果，特别是在存在资源竞争的场景下表现优异，为工作流能耗优化提供了可靠的基础。

Abstract: Scientific workflows are pipelines of interdependent tasks. They are increasingly executed on shared Kubernetes clusters via workflow engines such as Nextflow. Their energy consumption matters for both cost and sustainability. It is necessary to examine and optimize workflow tasks individually, because they can be very heterogeneous. However, estimating task-level energy on clusters is difficult: Intel RAPL counters report only node-level energy, access to counters and host process information is typically restricted, and concurrent workloads introduce resource contention and measurement noise. We present Nf-PEAK, a containerized method to attribute CPU-package and DRAM energy to individual processes and Nextflow tasks. Nf-PEAK (i) identifies workflow pods, (ii) maps pods to host processes via cgroup metadata, (iii) samples RAPL and per-process performance counters, and (iv) applies a non-linear energy-credit model before aggregating results at task level. On a Kubernetes cluster, we evaluate three nf-core workflows under controlled co-located CPU load. Nf-PEAK reaches an average Mean Absolute Percentage Error of 6.6% in isolated runs and 10.9% when an unrelated workload saturates 8 of 32 hardware threads per node, and remains stable across 2, 3, 4, and 8 nodes. Compared to the state-of-the-art Kubernetes tool Kepler, Nf-PEAK yields lower error on average, particularly under co-located load.

</details>


### [17] [Exploiting Multicast for Accelerating Collective Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22428)
*Chao Xu,Xu Zhang,Zihang Luo,Yuyan Wu,Guoxin Qian,Yufeng Yao,Chihyung Wang,Jingbin Zhou*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了MultiWrite，一种新型多对多传输语义，通过消除冗余数据包直接降低算子延迟。在Ascend NPU上实现并长期压力测试表明，基于MultiWrite的算子在商用部署设备上实现了高达33%的延迟降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大型模型训练和推理中，降低集合通信延迟是一个关键目标。现代并行化策略的核心组件AllGather和AlltoAll等通信依赖于基于单播的写入，会为多个接收者在物理链路上传输相同数据的重复副本，这种冗余传输导致网络瓶颈拥堵，降低端到端延迟。传统多播存在管理平面开销大和生态系统兼容性问题。

Method: 提出MultiWrite，一种新型多对多传输语义，采用多播原则来解决传统多播在AI工作负载中的关键限制，包括消除冗余数据包传输。在Ascend NPU上实现该方案。

Result: 长期压力测试表明，基于MultiWrite的算子在商用部署设备上实现了高达33%的延迟降低。

Conclusion: MultiWrite成功解决了传统多播在AI工作负载中的局限性，通过消除冗余数据包传输有效降低了多对多通信的延迟，在商用设备上验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Reducing collective communication latency is a critical goal for large model training and inference in both academia and industry. Many-to-many communications, such as AllGather and AlltoAll (dispatch), are core components of modern parallelization strategies. State-of-the-art implementations of these communications rely on unicast-based writes and transmit duplicate copies of the same data across physical links for multiple receivers. This redundant transmission congests network bottlenecks and degrades end-to-end latency. We present MultiWrite, a novel many-to-many transmission semantic that eliminates redundant packets to directly reduce operator latency. MultiWrite adopts multicast principles while addressing critical limitations of traditional multicast for AI workloads. These limitations include heavy management plane overhead and ecosystem compatibility issues. We implement MultiWrite on Ascend NPUs. Long-term stress tests demonstrate that our MultiWrite-based operators achieve up to 33% latency reduction on commercially deployed devices.

</details>


### [18] [Relay-Based Synchronization of Replicated Data Types in Opportunistic Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22491)
*Frédéric Guidec,Yves Mahéo*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究了在机会网络中利用移动中继节点加速基于状态的CRDT副本收敛的方法，新协议能显著改善收敛性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在机会网络中，信息传播仅依赖移动设备间短暂的成对无线接触，设计分布式应用具有挑战性。CRDT虽能帮助应对此挑战，但其副本能否收敛及收敛速度完全取决于设备间无线接触的发生情况。

Method: 提出利用移动中继作为提升机会网络中基于状态的CRDT副本收敛的手段，设计了允许副本与中继同步的新协议，并定义了新的度量标准来观察和表征副本的收敛情况。

Result: 仿真结果表明，使用中继可以显著改善副本的收敛性能，甚至在仅靠副本无法收敛的场景中使收敛成为可能。

Conclusion: 移动中继机制能有效提升机会网络中CRDT副本的收敛能力，为机会网络环境下的分布式应用设计提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: In Opportunistic Networks (OppNets), the dissemination of information can only rely on transient pairwise radio contacts between mobile devices (peers). Designing distributed applications that can run in such conditions is a challenge, but replicated data types, and in particular Conflict-free Replicated Data Types (CRDTs), can help meet this challenge. A CRDT is inherently replicated data type whose replicas can be updated locally, yet eventually converge thanks to an anti-entropy algorithm that allows all replicas to synchronize in the background. Whether the replicas of a CRDT can actually converge in an OppNet, and how fast they can converge, depend on the occurrence of radio contacts between mobile devices. In this paper we investigate the idea of using mobile relays as a means to boost the convergence of stated-based CRDT replicas in an OppNet. New protocols are presented that allow the synchronization of replicas and relays, and new metrics are defined to observe and characterize the convergence of replicas. Simulation results show that using relays can significantly improve this convergence, and even make it possible in scenarios where the replicas alone would be unable to converge.

</details>


### [19] [AI-Driven Multi-Region Provisioning for Cloud Services Using Spot Fleets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22778)
*Javier Fabra,Enrique Molina-Giménez,Pedro García-López*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种AI驱动的多云区域Spot实例集群配置服务，能够在部署前预测配置和价格，实现了99.79%的预测准确率和高达64%的成本节省。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云服务平台越来越依赖弹性基础设施支持动态工作负载。Spot实例提供折扣计算资源，但由于动态定价、资源可用性和中断风险在不同地理区域存在差异，引入了不确定性。AWS EC2 Spot Service虽然通过分配策略简化了集群配置，但无法在部署前估算成本，且限制在单一区域配置。

Method: 提出AI驱动的多云区域Spot集群配置服务，将配置计划监控与预测模型相结合，在启动前估算集群配置和价格，实现跨区域的成本感知部署决策，同时保持EC2 Spot Service的操作行为。

Result: 系统在高达1500个vCPU的集群上进行了验证。实验结果表明，与EC2 Spot Service相比，预测准确率达到99.79%，通过利用区域价格波动性，潜在成本节省高达64%。

Conclusion: 该AI驱动的配置服务成功解决了EC2 Spot Service在多云区域配置和成本预测方面的局限性，为云服务平台的成本优化和资源管理提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Cloud service platforms increasingly rely on elastic infrastructures to support dynamic workloads. Spot instances provide discounted computing resources but introduce uncertainty due to dynamic pricing, resource availability, and interruption risks that vary across geographical regions. In Amazon Web Services, the EC2 Spot Service simplifies fleet provisioning through allocation strategies, but it cannot estimate fleet costs before deployment and restricts provisioning to a single region. This paper presents an AI-driven provisioning service for multi-region spot fleets. The proposed approach combines monitoring of provisioning plans with predictive models to estimate fleet configurations and prices before launch, enabling cost-aware deployment decisions across regions while preserving the operational behavior of the EC2 Spot Service. The system was validated with fleets of up to 1500 vCPUs. Experimental results show a prediction accuracy of 99.79% compared to the EC2 Spot Service and potential cost savings of up to 64% by exploiting regional price variability.

</details>

### [20] [Benchmarking and Improving Monitors for Out-Of-Distribution Alignment Failure in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21602)
*Dylan Feng,Pragya Srivastava,Cassidy Laidlaw*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文引入了MOOD基准，用于检测大语言模型在分布外（OOD）场景下的对齐失败，并发现结合OOD检测器与安全分类器可以显著提升召回率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的安全与对齐失败通常发生在分布外场景中，例如模型开发者未曾预见的异常提示或响应模式，因此需要研究监控管道是否能有效检测这些分布外对齐失败。

Method: 提出MOOD基准，包含受限训练集和七个多样化的对齐失败测试集，测试了四种OOD检测器，并将安全分类器与马氏距离及基于困惑度的OOD检测器相结合。

Result: 安全分类器在分布外场景下泛化能力不足；结合OOD检测器后，召回率从39%提升至45%；在不同模型规模下，结合OOD检测的监控比单纯扩大安全分类器参数（20倍）获得更高的召回率增益。

Conclusion: OOD检测应成为大语言模型监控的关键组成部分，为后续研究提供了基础。

Abstract: Many safety and alignment failures of large language models (LLMs) occur due to out-of-distribution (OOD) situations: unusual prompt or response patterns that are unforeseen by model developers. We systematically study whether LLM monitoring pipelines can detect these OOD alignment failures by introducing a benchmark called Misalignment Out Of Distribution (MOOD). It is difficult to find failures that are truly OOD for off-the-shelf models trained on vast safety datasets. We sidestep this by including a restricted training set in MOOD that we use to train our own monitors, as well as seven test sets with diverse alignment failures that are outside the training distribution. Using MOOD, we find that guard models (safety classifiers) often fail to generalize OOD. To fix this, we propose combining guard models with OOD detectors. We test four types of OOD detectors and find that a combination of a guard model with Mahalanobis distance and perplexity-based OOD detectors can improve recall from 39% to 45%. We also establish positive scaling trends across model scales for monitors that combine a guard model and OOD detector; we find that incorporating OOD detection into monitoring achieves a higher recall gain than using a guard model with 20 times more parameters. Our work suggests that OOD detection should be a crucial component of LLM monitoring and provides a foundation for further work on this important problem.

</details>


### [21] [TO-Agents: A Multi-Agent AI Pipeline for Preference-Guided Topology Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21622)
*Isabella A. Stewart,Hongrui Chen,Faez Ahmed*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了TO-Agents多智能体AI框架，通过自然语言交互和多视角视觉语言推理，将设计师的高层意图自动转化为拓扑优化求解器参数，实现了从设计意图到增材制造原型的端到端自动化流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统拓扑优化需要设计师手动将定性意图（如视觉风格、产品体验、可制造性）转化为求解器参数设置，这些参数与设计偏好之间缺乏直接联系，导致设计师陷入底层参数调优工作。

Method: 开发了一个多智能体AI框架，包含：(1) 将自然语言问题描述转化为求解器输入；(2) 运行拓扑优化求解器；(3) 渲染3D拓扑结构；(4) 使用多视角视觉语言推理和独立评判智能体对结果进行评估并修正参数。框架还包含制造智能体对设计进行后处理以适应增材制造。

Result: 在悬臂梁和手机支架两个长期设计任务中，系统在60%的试验中成功生成符合审美偏好（自然树形分叉结构）的设计，相比无视觉或历史反馈的基线方法成功率提升高达6倍。系统展现出识别有效参数、从不良修正中恢复、扩展设计探索空间的能力，并能实现端到端的意图到原型设计。

Conclusion: 智能体拓扑优化能够将设计师从底层参数调优转向高层形态与功能规范，但也识别出过冲、选择性记忆、工具误用、参数推理错误等失败模式，需要在自主工程设计中建立保障措施以确保可靠性。

Abstract: Topology optimization can generate efficient structures, but designers often must manually translate qualitative intent, such as desired visual style, product experience, or manufacturability into solver settings that are not directly tied to those preferences. We present TO-Agents, a multi-agent AI framework that connects natural-language design intent with iterative topology optimization. The framework converts a human-provided problem description into validated solver inputs, runs a topology optimization solver, renders the resulting 3D topology, and uses multi-view vision-language reasoning with an independent judge agent to critique each result and revise solver parameters. We evaluate the framework on two long-horizon design tasks: a cantilever beam benchmark and a phone-stand product design. In both tasks, the designer specifies an aesthetic preference for hierarchically branched structures inspired by natural tree morphologies, and the system performs four revision cycles across ten independent replicates. TO-Agents produces at least one preference-aligned design in 60% of trials for each case study, corresponding to up to 6x more successful trials than an ablated pipeline without visual or historical feedback. Judge scores and human evaluations show that the pipeline can identify effective parameter levers, recover from poor revisions, and expand design exploration. A manufacturing agent further post-processes top-ranked designs for additive manufacturing, enabling end-to-end intent-to-prototype design. We also identify failure modes, including overshooting, selective memory, misplaced tools, and incorrect parameter reasoning. These results suggest that agentic topology optimization can shift designers from low-level parameter tuning toward higher-level specification of form and function, while highlighting safeguards needed for reliable autonomous engineering design.

</details>


### [22] [The Shape of Testimony: A Scalable Framework for Oral History Archive Comparison](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21623)
*Itamar Trainin,Renana Keydar,Amit Pinchevski*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究通过计算分析方法验证了大屠杀幸存者证词的结构差异，发现两种档案风格存在重叠，挑战了简单的二元划分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大屠杀研究领域长期存在一个普遍假设：USC肖基金会档案采用结构化、访谈者引导的证词收集方式，而耶鲁Fortunoff视频档案更倾向于自由开放的形式。这一区分影响了学术研究和后续档案建设。研究者希望批判性地检验这一主张的有效性。

Method: 研究对两个档案库中超过1,600份证词进行大规模计算分析。采用话语分割、主题建模和大语言模型(LLM)分析技术，通过主题连贯性、访谈者-幸存者互动动态以及问题类型分布等指标来量化证词的"结构化"程度。

Result: 结果总体上证实了早期研究识别的结构差异，同时也揭示了两个档案库之间存在显著重叠。这些重叠既体现在个别访谈内部，也表现在共同的叙事模式中，这使通常应用于这些口述历史的简单"结构化vs.自由形式"二分法变得复杂化。

Conclusion: 研究不仅重新审视了大屠杀研究的奠基性主张，还为比较语料库分析提供了可扩展、可复制的框架。作为概念验证，这项工作为数字口述历史、叙事分析和公民科学标注平台的设计提出了更广泛的应用前景。

Abstract: Researchers in Holocaust studies have often distinguished between two styles of oral survivor testimony: the USC Shoah Foundation's interviews tend to follow a structured, interviewer-guided format, whereas the Yale Fortunoff Video Archive generally favors a more free-form, open-ended style. This distinction has influenced both scholarly research and the development of later archives. In this study, we critically examine that claim by conducting a large-scale computational analysis of more than 1,600 testimonies from both collections. Leveraging discourse segmentation, topic modeling, and large language model (LLM) based analysis, we quantify the "structuredness" level of testimonies through topic coherence, interviewer-survivor dynamics, and the distribution of question types. Our results generally corroborate the structural differences identified in earlier research, while also revealing significant overlaps between the collections, both within individual interviews and across common narrative patterns. This complicates the simple "structured vs. free-form" dichotomy often applied to these oral histories. Beyond revisiting a foundational claim in Holocaust studies, our work provides a scalable, replicable framework for comparative corpus analysis. As a proof of concept, it suggests broader applications for digital oral history, narrative analysis, and the design of citizen-science annotation platforms.

</details>


### [23] [MindLoom: Composing Thought Modes for Frontier-Level Reasoning Data Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21630)
*Haiyang Shen,Taian Guo,Xuanzhong Chen,Mugeng Liu,Weichen Bi,Wenchun Jing,Sixiong Xie,Zhuofan Shi,Yudong Han,Chongyang Pan,Siqi Zhong,Jinsheng Huang,Ming Zhang,Yun Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了MindLoom框架，通过组合式思维模式工程来合成前沿级推理数据，在9个基准测试上取得了优于基模型的性能表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理数据合成方法难以系统性地生成前沿级推理数据，对控制问题难度的结构因素缺乏深入理解，导致生成的数据多样性有限且难度控制不稳定。

Method: 提出MindLoom框架：1）将困难问题的解分解为思维模式链；2）训练检索模型匹配问题状态与思维模式；3）通过迭代应用检索到的思维模式合成新问题；4）使用基于rollout的评判阶段标注难度并提供正确响应用于监督微调。

Result: 在覆盖5个STEM学科和4个数学推理任务的9个基准测试上，使用MindLoom生成数据微调的模型在性能上优于基础模型、知识蒸馏和外部数据基线。消融研究证实了各组件的贡献，分析表明MindLoom覆盖了广泛的推理模式并保持了有效的难度控制。

Conclusion: MindLoom通过组合式思维模式工程成功实现了前沿级推理数据的系统化合成，为提升大语言模型的推理能力提供了一种有效的方法，代码已开源。

Abstract: Although LLMs have made substantial progress in reasoning, systematically producing frontier-level reasoning data remains difficult. Existing synthesis methods often have limited visibility into the structural factors that govern problem difficulty, which can result in narrow diversity and unstable difficulty control. In this work, we view the difficulty of a reasoning problem as arising from the accumulation of atomic knowledge-reasoning transformations, which we term thought modes. Building on this perspective, we propose MindLoom, a framework for synthesizing frontier-level reasoning data through compositional thought mode engineering. Given a collection of hard problems with verified solutions, MindLoom first decomposes those solutions into thought mode chains that reveal each problem's construction logic. It then trains a retrieval model that matches problem states to compatible thought modes, providing guidance on which reasoning challenges to introduce during synthesis. New problems are composed by iteratively applying retrieved thought modes to seed questions, with distribution-aligned sampling to encourage diverse reasoning coverage. Finally, a rollout-based judging stage labels generated questions by difficulty and supplies judged-correct responses for supervised fine-tuning. We evaluate MindLoom on nine benchmarks covering five STEM disciplines and four mathematical reasoning tasks across multiple model families and sizes. Models fine-tuned on MindLoom-generated data achieves favorable performances over base models, distillation, and external-data baselines across the reported benchmarks. Ablation studies indicate the contribution of each component, and further analysis suggests that MindLoom covers a broad range of reasoning patterns while maintaining useful difficulty control. We have open-sourced our implementation at https://github.com/EachSheep/MindLoom.

</details>


### [24] [AOP-Wiki EMOD 3.0: Data Model Expansions and Content Evaluation Framework for Using Agentic AI to Improve Integration between AOPs and New Approach Methodologies (NAMs)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21645)
*Virginia K. Hench,J. Harry Caufield,Sierra A. T. Moxon,Jason M. O'Brien,Stephen W. Edwards*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了AOP-Wiki EMOD 3.0证据模型原型，旨在通过数据模型扩展和AI技术现代化AOP-Wiki平台，以支持计算生成的AOP和定量AOP（qAOPs），服务于监管科学、生物医学和同一健康领域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AOP-Wiki的数据模型和应用基础设施存在局限性，限制了AOP的持续增长和发展。同时，代理式AI（agentic AI）的出现为AOP-Wiki数据现代化提供了新机遇，核心AOP原理可用于指导AI聚合和结构化AOP相关信息。

Method: 开发了AOP-Wiki EMOD 3.0，这是证据模型原型的第三个版本。重点关注三个核心解决方案：(1) AOP-Wiki内部质量改进；(2) 证据结构化以增强AOP的FAIR原则（可查找、可访问、可互操作、可重用）和AI就绪性；(3) 改进AOP框架与NAMs（新方法论）之间的整合。

Result: 具体展示了数据模型扩展成果，提出了AOP-Wiki转型的愿景，使其更好地服务于监管科学以及AOP在生物医学和同一健康（One Health）背景下的新兴应用。为支持计算生成的AOP和定量AOP（qAOPs）奠定了基础。

Conclusion: AOP-Wiki EMOD 3.0为下一代风险评估提供了改进的基础设施，通过质量提升、FAIR化和AI就绪性增强，以及AOP框架与NAMs的更好整合，将推动AOP在监管科学和生物医学领域的应用发展。

Abstract: Adverse Outcome Pathways (AOP) are logic models that causally link biological mechanisms that can be measured in a lab to adverse outcomes, relevant to chemical regulatory endpoints. AOPs contextualize new approach methodologies (NAMs), in vitro and in silico methods used as alternatives to animal testing and the sequential events in an AOP serve as multi-scale models spanning biological scales. The AOP-Wiki serves as the global repository for AOPs. While the AOP-Wiki has played a central role in AOP expansion over the past decade, constraints within the current data model and application infrastructure limit the AOP-Wiki from supporting continued AOP growth and evolution. Yet, the transformative power of agentic AI has re-invigorated AOP-Wiki data modernization efforts at a time when core AOP principles can be harnessed to inform use of AI for aggregating and structuring AOP-relevant information. Seizing upon this momentum, we present AOP-Wiki EMOD 3.0, the third in a series of evidence model prototypes, which concretely demonstrates data model expansions and our vision for how the AOP-Wiki might be transformed to better serve regulatory science and emergent use of AOPs in biomedical and One Health contexts. We aim to lay a foundation to support computationally-generated AOPs and quantitative AOPs (qAOPs) by focussing on solutions for AOP-Wiki internal quality improvement, evidence structuring to enhance AOP FAIRness and AI-readiness, and improved integration between the AOP framework and NAMs to better serve next generation risk assessment.

</details>


### [25] [Investigating Concept Alignment Using Implausible Category Members](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21683)
*Sunayana Rane,Brenden M. Lake,Thomas L. Griffiths*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过询问不合理类别成员（如“橄榄是交通工具吗？”）来探测AI系统的概念边界，发现AI在多个基本概念上与人类存在显著且意外的差异，这些概念错位会导致影响AI安全的下游问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发具有类人概念理解能力的AI系统对于构建安全、可靠且行为可解释的系统至关重要。传统的探测方法（询问合理的类别成员）容易使模型回忆训练数据模式，因此需要一种新策略来真正评估模型的概念级知识。

Method: 采用反向探测策略，通过询问不合理的类别成员来界定概念边界。使用Rosch和 Mervis的经典心理学研究中的对象，让AI系统将对象分配到上级类别和错配的上级类别，并与人类参与者在全范围的类别内和跨类别分配任务上的表现进行比较。

Result: 研究发现AI模型在多个概念上与人类存在显著差异：将“词语”归类为“交通工具”和“衣物”；将多个“蔬菜”成员识别为“水果”；将非武器类别的样例分配到“武器”类别。这些概念错位会转化为有问题的下游行为。

Conclusion: AI系统在基本概念边界上与人类存在意想不到的差异，这些概念错位会对AI安全产生重要影响，表明在开发可靠的AI系统时需要更深入地理解和解决概念对齐问题。

Abstract: Developing AI systems with a human-like understanding of everyday concepts is a key step towards developing safe, reliable systems whose behavior makes sense to humans. When probing concept understanding, asking questions about plausible category members (e.g., "Is a car a vehicle?") is likely to recall patterns in the model's vast training data. We pursue an alternative strategy, characterizing the boundaries of conceptual categories by asking about implausible category members (e.g., "Is an olive a vehicle?") to probe the kind of concept-level knowledge we take for granted in fellow humans. We characterize concept boundaries for a set of fundamental concepts by studying AI systems' assignments of objects to superordinate categories from a classic psychological study by Rosch and Mervis, as well as their assignments of the same objects to mismatched superordinate categories. We compare these assignments to those made by human participants on the full range of within-category and cross-category assignment tasks. Our results reveal a range of concepts for which which models differ in meaningful and surprising ways from humans, including treating "words" as belonging to categories like "vehicles" and "clothing," identifying several "vegetable" category members as "fruit," and assigning exemplars from non-weapon categories to the "weapons" category. We also demonstrate how these instances of concept misalignment translate into problematic downstream behavior with implications for AI safety.

</details>


### [26] [The Impact of AI Usage and Informativeness on Skill Development in Logical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21695)
*Shang Wu,Hongyu Yao,Catarina Belem,Shuyuan Fu,Mark Steyvers,Padhraic Smyth*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究探讨AI辅助对个体技能发展的影响，发现AI使用量与技能学习呈负相关，但这种效应受AI信息量的调节：低信息量AI会损害学习，而高信息量AI能提升短期表现且不损害长期技能，表明需根据场景合理管控AI的使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着人工智能在人类问题解决中的广泛应用，其对个体技能发展的具体影响尚不明确。研究者希望理解AI的使用模式和信息特征如何影响学习过程，以便为AI辅助环境下的技能培养提供指导。

Method: 采用受控实验设计，在逻辑推理任务中提供按需AI辅助，追踪不同AI使用强度（重度/轻度用户）的个体表现，并对比AI移除后的表现变化。同时考察AI信息量（低信息量/高信息量）的调节作用。

Result: 1）AI使用量越大，技能发展越弱，重度AI用户表现劣于对照组；2）低信息量AI既无助于即时表现，也损害后续学习；3）高信息量AI提升短期表现且平均不损害后续技能，但存在异质性效应；4）AI既可能作为独立推理的补充，也可能成为替代品削弱推理能力。

Conclusion: AI对技能发展的影响具有情境依赖性，需通过调控AI的可及性和使用方式来平衡即时表现提升与长期技能培养，这对AI辅助环境下的教育和工作培训具有重要政策意义。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) is being increasingly integrated into human problem-solving, yet its effects on individual skill development remain unclear. We examine how both AI usage and informativeness can shape learning in the context of a controlled logical reasoning task with on-demand access to AI assistance. We find that greater AI usage is associated with weaker skill development: heavy AI users underperform relative to comparable peers, whereas light AI users perform similarly to matched users who do not use AI. We also find in our study that these patterns are mediated by AI informativeness. Low-information AI neither improves immediate performance nor preserves performance after AI assistance is removed, and is linked to weaker learning overall. On the other hand, high-information AI was found to improve short-run performance without reducing post-AI outcomes on average in our experiments, but with heterogeneous effects. Our findings in general suggest that AI can, depending on context, either complement human skill development by amplifying independent reasoning or can act as a substitute that undermines such reasoning, with the implication that regulating AI access and usage will be important for promoting skill development in the presence of AI assistance.

</details>

*Giorgio Piras,Raffaele Mura,Fabio Brau,Maura Pintor,Luca Oneto,Fabio Roli,Battista Biggio*

TL;DR: 本文将拒绝抑制重新表述为针对线性探针的潜在空间规避攻击，揭示了现有方法的理论局限，并提出了一种新的控制潜在空间规避攻击，在多个模型上实现了最先进的攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有通过消融拒绝方向来抑制语言模型拒绝行为的方法缺乏对潜在空间变换的理论解释，且仅停留在决策边界上，无法充分推动表示进入合规区域，亟需一种既有理论依据又能突破边界限制的新方法。

Method: 将拒绝抑制重构为对线性探针的潜在空间规避攻击；指出差分均值方向定义了探针，消融操作等同于投影至决策边界；提出控制潜在空间规避攻击，通过优化的置信度将表示投影越过边界、推入合规区域。

Result: 在15个指令调优、多模态和推理模型上，所提方法实现了最先进的攻击成功率，优于现有的拒绝消融基线和专门的越狱攻击。

Conclusion: 本研究为拒绝抑制提供了原则性的理论框架，揭示了现有方法的局限，并通过将表示进一步推向合规区域，显著提升了攻击效果。

Abstract: Safety-aligned language models are trained to refuse harmful requests, yet refusal behavior can be suppressed by steering their internal representations. Existing methods do so by ablating a refusal direction from model activations, aiming to remove refusal from the model's residual stream. Despite their empirical success, these methods lack a principled account of the latent-space transformation they induce and why it suppresses refusal. In this work, we recast refusal suppression as a latent-space evasion attack against linear probes trained to separate refused from answered prompts. Under this view, prior work's difference-in-means direction naturally defines such a probe, and its ablation is exactly a projection onto its decision boundary, i.e., a minimum-confidence evasion attack. This perspective not only explains the empirical success of prior work but also admits a key limitation: evasion stops at the decision boundary, motivating the need to push representations further into the compliant region, i.e., where the model answers. We leverage this by proposing a Controlled Latent-space Evasion attack that projects representations past the boundary with an optimized confidence. We achieve state-of-the-art attack success rate across 15 instruction-tuned, multimodal, and reasoning models, outperforming existing refusal-ablation baselines and specialized jailbreak attacks.

</details>

*Zijian Du,Nathaniel Pinckney*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了Trace2Skill框架，通过将硬件代理的自然语言技能视为可演化策略，利用运行轨迹挖掘成功/失败模式并转换为诊断和经验教训，结合密集验证器反馈，在无需RTL专用模型微调的情况下显著提升了复杂Verilog设计问题的任务通过率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂Verilog设计问题（CVDP）对硬件LLM代理构成挑战，因为解决这些问题需要在大型仓库快照中定位验证器相关的RTL、测试平台、包含路径和构建依赖关系，进行精确编辑，并从稀疏的隐藏验证器故障中恢复。现有的最终通过/失败标签对于困难故障来说过于粗糙，且缺乏有效的反馈机制。

Method: Trace2Skill是一个测试时扩展框架，将代理的自然语言技能视为可演化策略。该方法：(1)挖掘重复的运行轨迹以识别成功和失败模式；(2)将其转换为密集诊断和oracle教训；(3)使用oracle、变异器和选择器循环生成任务特定技能来指导后续的搜索、编辑、验证和恢复；(4)支持有界运行时密集验证器反馈，返回净化的功能观测同时保持隐藏工具和参考解决方案不可访问。

Result: 在击败种子CVDP代理的困难CVDP任务上（包括也击败前沿编码代理的任务），Trace2Skill配合密集验证器反馈显著提高了任务通过率，并在先前未解决的任务上实现了突破性通过，无需高质量微调数据、专用RTL模型训练或模型权重更新。

Conclusion: Trace2Skill提供了一个通用的测试时扩展策略，无需模型微调即可显著提升硬件代理在复杂Verilog设计问题上的性能。该框架具有良好的泛化性，可以扩展到数字设计之外的其他可验证EDA任务中。

Abstract: Complex Verilog Design Problems (CVDP) challenge hardware LLM agents because solving them requires localizing verifier-relevant RTL, testbenches, include paths, and build dependencies inside large repository snapshots, making precise edits, and recovering from sparse hidden-verifier failures. We present Trace2Skill, a test-time scaling framework that improves a hardware agent without RTL-specialized model fine-tuning. Rather than training a new model or only sampling more candidate solutions, Trace2Skill treats the agent's natural-language skill as an evolvable policy. It mines repeated rollout traces for success and failure modes, converts them into dense diagnostics and oracle lessons, and uses an oracle, mutator, and selector loop to produce task-specific skills that guide later search, editing, validation, and recovery. Because final pass/fail labels are often too coarse for hard failures, Trace2Skill also supports bounded runtime dense verifier feedback that returns sanitized functional observations while keeping hidden harnesses and reference solutions inaccessible to the agent. This feedback helps guide skill evolution and agent execution by connecting skill text, verifier evidence, and downstream behavior. Across hard CVDP tasks that defeat the seed CVDP agent, including tasks that also defeat frontier coding agents, Trace2Skill with dense verifier feedback substantially improves task pass rates and produces breakthrough passes on previously unsolved tasks, without requiring high-quality fine-tuning data, specialized RTL model training, or model weight updates. The same framework provides a general test-time scaling strategy that can extend beyond digital design to other verifiable EDA tasks.

</details>


### [29] [AttuneBench: A Conversation-Based Benchmark for LLM Emotional Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21739)
*Kate M. Lubrano,Faisal Sayed,Ankita Rathod,Akshansh,Craver Corbyn Thomas-Smith,Mark E. Whiting,Karina Nguyen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了AttuneBench，一个基于真实多轮对话的情感智能评估基准，发现情感识别、行为分类、偏好预测和响应质量判断是相互独立的能力，偏好对齐比情感标签准确率更能区分模型能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的情感智能（EI）基准依赖于合成提示、单轮对话或第三方标注，无法直接测量模型在真实对话过程中如何推断和响应用户的情感状态。随着LLM在日常生活中承担对话角色，评估其情感智能能力变得日益重要。

Method: 构建了AttuneBench基准，基于200个真实的人机多轮对话数据，参与者在对话过程中提供逐轮的情感状态、模型行为和偏好响应的标注，对11个模型在情感识别、行为分类、偏好预测和响应质量判断四个维度进行评估。

Result: 模型在情感识别、行为分类、偏好预测和响应质量判断四个任务上的排名基本相互独立，表明情感智能行为可分解为多个独立能力。偏好对齐和响应质量判断比情感标签准确率更能区分模型能力。情感智能行为需要预测特定用户在具体情境下想要什么样的响应。

Conclusion: AttuneBench提供了一个评估情感智能各项能力和诊断模型在情感显著对话中优势和失败模式的框架，揭示了单轮或合成格式无法直接捕捉跨轮对话中用户特定偏好的局限性。

Abstract: Emotional intelligence (EI), the ability to perceive, understand, and respond appropriately to others' emotional states, is central to human communication, and increasingly important to assess as LLMs assume conversational roles in everyday life. Existing EI benchmarks rely on synthetic prompts, single-turn cases, or third-party annotation. These approaches do not directly measure how models infer and respond to a participant's emotional state over the course of a real conversation. We introduce AttuneBench, a benchmark grounded in 200 genuine multi-turn human-model conversations in which participants conversed with anonymized LLMs and provided turn-by-turn annotations of their emotional state, the model's behavior, and their preferred responses. Across 11 evaluated models, we find that model rankings on emotion recognition, behavioral classification, preference prediction, and judged response quality are largely independent, indicating that emotionally intelligent behavior decomposes into separable capabilities. Preference alignment and response-quality judgments are substantially more model-discriminating than emotion-label accuracy. These results indicate that emotionally intelligent behavior requires predicting what kind of response a specific user wants in context, a distinction that aggregate scoring can obscure and that single-turn or synthetic formats cannot directly capture across turns. AttuneBench provides a framework for assessing each of these capabilities and for diagnosing model-specific strengths and failure modes in emotionally salient conversation.

</details>
