*Priyamvada Tripathi,Bill Kapralos*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文综述了AI在严肃游戏中支持实时教学适应的潜力，区分了教学智能与适应性，分析了LLMs、强化学习和智能体架构的应用前景及面临的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 严肃游戏在医疗、国防和教育等领域广泛应用，但面临场景设计静态、创作瓶颈、学习者建模有限和实时教学适应困难等持续挑战。尽管AI技术带来了动态场景变化、情境反馈和适应性节奏等新能力，但AI集成也引发了有效性、透明度、系统控制和学习者信任等关键问题。

Method: 通过历史综合分析法，追溯自适应学习系统的发展历程，从早期的计算机辅助教学到智能辅导系统(ITS)、动态难度调整(DDA)、创作平台、学习分析以及最新的AI架构。在此基础上，探讨大语言模型(LLMs)、强化学习(RL)和智能体架构如何为严肃游戏提供更整合的智能性和适应性。

Result: 明确了教学智能（系统推断学习者知识并推理教学适当响应的能力）与适应性（在交互过程中修改教学行为的能力）的概念区分。识别了AI赋能严肃游戏的实际和研究挑战，包括可解释性、验证、计算成本以及长期学习效果实证证据有限等问题。

Conclusion: 当代AI方法有望解决严肃游戏中的教学适应挑战，但在实现有效的AI集成时需要权衡有效性、透明度、计算成本和学习效果验证等多重因素，未来需要更多关于长期学习效果的实证研究。

Abstract: Serious games are widely used for learning and training across domains such as healthcare, defense, and education. Persistent challenges remain, however, including static scenario design, authoring bottlenecks, limited learner modeling, and difficulty implementing meaningful real-time instructional adaptation. Recent advances in artificial intelligence (AI) introduce novel capabilities such as dynamic scenario variation, contextual feedback, adaptive pacing, and learner-state modeling that may help address some of these limitations. At the same time, integrating AI into serious games raises important questions related to validity, transparency, system control, and learner trust. This chapter examines how contemporary AI approaches may support real-time instructional adaptation in serious games. It distinguishes between instructional intelligence, defined as a system's capacity to infer learner knowledge and reason about pedagogically appropriate responses, and adaptivity, defined as the ability to modify instructional actions during interaction. A historical synthesis of adaptive learning systems is presented, tracing developments from early computer-assisted instruction through intelligent tutoring systems (ITS), dynamic difficulty adjustment (DDA), authoring platforms, learning analytics, and recent AI-enabled architectures. Building on this perspective, the chapter discusses how large language models (LLMs), reinforcement learning (RL), and agent-based architectures may contribute to more integrated forms of intelligence and adaptivity in serious games. It also highlights practical and research challenges associated with AI-enabled systems, including explainability, validation, computational cost, and the limited empirical evidence regarding long-term learning outcomes in AI-enabled serious games.

</details>


### [31] [SMDD-Bench: Can LLMs Solve Real-World Small Molecule Drug Design Tasks?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21740)
*Kevin Han,Renfei Zhang,Kathy Wei,Hamed Mahdavi,Niloofar Mireshghallah,Amir Farimani*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了SMDD-Bench，一个用于评估大语言模型（LLM）智能体在小分子药物设计任务中性能的标准化基准测试，包含502个任务实例，涵盖5种任务类型。测试结果显示，表现最好的模型GPT-5.4仅能解决40.2%的任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前对LLM智能体在小分子药物设计（SMDD）任务上的评估方法存在局限性：要么是临时性的，要么过于简单不适合真实世界的发现任务，要么规模受限，或者局限于单轮问答。因此需要建立一个标准化的、具有挑战性的基准测试来全面评估LLM智能体在药物设计中的实际能力。

Method: 研究者构建了SMDD-Bench基准测试，这是一个多轮、长视界的智能体基准，包含502个保证可解的任务实例，涵盖5种任务类型：2D药效团识别、相互作用点发现、骨架跃迁、先导化合物优化和片段组装。任务覆盖广阔的化学空间，涉及102个独特的蛋白质靶点。研究对7个前沿的开源和闭源LLM进行了基准测试。

Result: 在所有测试的模型中，表现最好的GPT-5.4仅能解决40.2%的任务。这表明当前最先进的LLM在完成真实世界的小分子药物设计任务方面仍面临重大挑战，需要具备强大的化学和生物推理能力、3D直觉、专业工具使用理解以及在有限oracle调用次数下的规划专业知识。

Conclusion: SMDD-Bench为评估LLM智能体在计算药物设计领域的能力提供了一个标准化的测试平台，旨在推动该领域朝着训练和评估完全自主的计算药物设计LLM智能体的方向发展。研究团队已在smddbench.com上公开了排行榜。

Abstract: LLM agents have incredible potential for scientific discovery applications. However, the performance of LLM agents on real-world, small molecule drug design (SMDD) tasks across diverse chemistries and targets is unclear. Current evaluation methods are either ad hoc, too simple for real-world discovery, limited in scale, or restricted to single-turn question answering. In effort to standardize the evaluation of LLM agents on small molecule design, we introduce SMDD-Bench, a challenging, multi-turn, long-horizon agentic benchmark consisting of 502 guaranteed-solvable task instances spanning 5 task types: 2D Pharmacophore Identification, Interaction Point Discovery, Scaffold Hopping, Lead Optimization, and Fragment Assembly. SMDD-Bench tasks span a wide region of chemical space and involve 102 unique protein targets. Completely solving the benchmark would require having strong chemical and biological reasoning and 3D intuition, understanding specialized tool use, and displaying planning expertise over a limited number of oracle calls. We benchmark 7 frontier open and closed source LLMs and find even the most performant LLM, GPT5.4, solves only 40.2\% of tasks. We hope SMDD-Bench provides a standardized testbed to invigorate the field towards training and evaluating LLM agents for fully autonomous computational drug design. We host a public leaderboard at smddbench.com .

</details>


### [32] [The Log is the Agent: Event-Sourced Reactive Graphs for Auditable, Forkable Agentic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21997)
*Yohei Nakajima*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ActiveGraph是一种新的Agent运行时框架，它反转了传统框架的设计顺序：以追加式事件日志为事实来源，工作图作为日志的确定性投影，通过共享图实现组件协调，提供确定性重放、低成本分支和端到端血缘追踪三大特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Agent框架通常以语言模型为核心，对话循环优先，工具和规则次之，日志层作为事后补充，状态持久化为可检索的"记忆"。这种设计缺乏确定性重放、低成本分支和完整血缘追踪能力，无法满足复杂Agent系统的需求。

Method: 提出ActiveGraph运行时架构：以追加式事件日志作为唯一事实来源；工作图是日志的确定性投影；行为（函数、类、LLM例程或附加在类型边上的逻辑）响应图的变化并发出新事件；组件间无直接指令，通过共享图进行协调；引入确定性契约确保重放的正确性。

Result: 该设计实现了三个传统检索-摘要记忆系统无法提供的特性：(1)从日志确定性重放任意运行；(2)低成本分支，可在任意事件处分支运行而无需重新执行共享前缀；(3)从高层目标到产生每个制品的具体模型调用的端到端血缘追踪。通过尽职调查示例验证了可从日志重建完整因果结构。

Conclusion: ActiveGraph通过反转传统框架的设计顺序，提供了一种更适合自改进Agent的底层架构。该框架扩展了BabyAGI谱系和图记忆研究，为构建具有确定性重放、低成本分支和完整血缘追踪能力的Agent系统提供了新思路。

Abstract: Most agent frameworks are built around the language model: a conversation loop comes first, then tools, then rules, and finally a logging layer bolted on for observability, with state persisted as retrievable "memory." We describe ActiveGraph, a runtime that inverts this arrangement. The append-only event log is the source of truth; the working graph is a deterministic projection of that log; and behaviors--ordinary functions, classes, LLM-backed routines, or logic attached to typed edges--react to changes in the graph and emit new events. No component instructs another; coordination happens entirely through the shared graph. This single design decision yields three properties that retrieval-and-summarization memory systems do not provide: deterministic replay of any run from its log, cheap forking that branches a run at any event without re-executing the shared prefix, and end-to-end lineage from a high-level goal down to the individual model call that produced each artifact. We present the architecture, a determinism contract that makes replay sound, and a worked diligence example whose full causal structure is reconstructable from the log alone. We discuss--without claiming to demonstrate--why this substrate is unusually well suited to self-improving agents, and how it extends the BabyAGI lineage and prior graph-memory research.

</details>


### [33] [Who Uses AI? Platforms, Workforce, and AI Exposure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21743)
*Michelle Yin,Burhan Ogut*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现使用AI平台对话日志测量职业暴露度存在显著测量误差，主要反映平台用户群而非劳动力市场特征，导致就业效应估计严重偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献越来越多地使用人工智能平台对话日志来测量职业对AI的暴露程度，但这种方法的有效性尚未得到充分验证。作者发现这些暴露度评分可能部分反映了平台用户基础特征，而非真实的劳动力市场情况，这可能导致对AI就业效应的估计产生严重偏差。

Method: 作者采用控制变量法，保持结果变量、样本、控制变量和估计量不变，仅改变平台输入数据源，比较不同平台产生的估计结果差异。同时使用美国劳工统计局（BLS）的劳动力市场份额对数据进行重新加权处理。作者还推导了非经典测量误差的概率极限和部分识别界限。

Result: 改变平台输入会导致ChatGPT发布后就业系数变化1.9倍；同一供应商的消费者渠道与企业渠道产生符号相反的估计结果；使用BLS劳动力市场份额重新加权后，估计值衰减42%至93%。测量误差偏差导致对替代效应的低估程度大于对增强效应的低估。

Conclusion: 使用AI平台对话日志测量职业暴露度存在非经典测量误差问题，这种误差部分反映了平台用户群特征而非劳动力市场特征，导致AI就业效应估计出现严重偏差。研究为这类测量误差提供了理论框架和校正方法。

Abstract: A growing literature uses artificial intelligence platform conversation logs to measure occupation exposure. We show that these scores partly measure platform user base rather than the workforce. Holding outcome, sample, controls, and estimator fixed while varying only the platform input changes the post-ChatGPT employment coefficient by a factor of 1.9, and within-vendor consumer-versus-enterprise channels produce estimates that disagree in sign. Reweighting to Bureau of Labor Statistics workforce shares attenuates estimates by 42 to 93 percent. We formalize the non-classical measurement error, derive probability limits and partial-identification bounds for employment elasticities. The bias understates substitution more than augmentation.

</details>


### [34] [Cross-domain benchmarks reveal when coordinated AI agents improve scientific inference from partial evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22300)
*Fiona Y. Wong,Markus J. Buehler*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究构建了一个跨领域的科学任务基准测试，用于评估协调AI代理相对于简单科学工作流程的价值。结果显示：跨渠道组合在气候-媒介疾病和系外行星验证任务中表现优异（AUROC分别达0.944和0.955），但在某些情况下与强力基线持平；当单一信号主导时，协调主要改善可解释性和可追溯性；基准仅在有明确比较器支持时才认可协调的价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学证据通常跨越多个仪器、数据库和学科，没有任何单一来源能够记录完整的现象。这使得确定协调AI代理何时能比更简单的科学工作流程增加价值变得困难。研究旨在系统性地评估协调AI代理在不同科学场景中的实际价值。

Method: 构建涵盖四个科学任务的跨领域基准测试：分子结构到音乐表征的映射、科学历史范式转移检测、媒介传播疾病出现识别、以及凌日系外行星候选体审查。每个案例均采用冻结评估小组、预定义评分协议、明确基线、消融实验或零对照，并说明局限性。使用ScienceClaw x Infinite提供可审计的工件和溯源层。

Result: 定义了三种运行机制：1）当不同学科各捕获现象的一部分时，跨渠道组合优于单渠道基线（气候-媒介疾病AUROC 0.944，系外行星审查AUROC 0.955）；2）但系外行星工作流与强力组合摘要基线基本持平，说明分解并不总能提升顶级性能；3）当单一信号主导时（如范式转移检测），协调主要改善解释性和可追溯性；4）对于分子声化，收益在于表征而非预测。

Conclusion: 基准测试仅当相应的性能、溯源或表征主张得到明确比较器支持时，才会认可协调的价值。研究为评估AI协调在科学研究中的实际效用提供了系统化的方法论框架，避免了对AI代理价值的不当夸大。

Abstract: Scientific evidence often spans instruments, databases, and disciplines, so no single source records the full phenomenon. This makes it difficult to determine when coordinated AI agents add value over simpler scientific workflows. We evaluate this question with a cross-domain benchmark spanning four scientific tasks: mapping molecular structure into musical representations, detecting historical paradigm shifts in science, identifying vector-borne disease emergence, and vetting transiting-exoplanet candidates. Each case uses a frozen evaluation panel, predefined scoring protocols, explicit baselines, ablations or null controls, and stated limitations. The results define three operating regimes. When different disciplines each capture only part of the phenomenon, cross-channel composites improve over single-channel baselines: climate-vector emergence reaches AUROC 0.944 and exoplanet vetting reaches AUROC 0.955. However, the exoplanet workflow is effectively tied with a strong combined-summary baseline, showing that decomposition does not always improve top-line performance. When one signal dominates, as in paradigm-shift detection, coordination mainly improves interpretation and traceability. For molecular sonification, the gain is representational rather than predictive. ScienceClaw x Infinite provides the auditable artifact and provenance layer for this evaluation. The benchmark therefore assigns value to coordination only when the corresponding performance, provenance, or representation claim is supported by explicit comparators.

</details>


### [35] [A Causal Argumentation Method for Explainability of Machine Learning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21758)
*Henry Salgado,Meagan R. Kendall,Martine Ceberio*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合因果发现与论辩推理的新方法，用于解释AI模型为何做出特定决策，而非仅仅识别相关特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的可解释AI方法通常只能识别与模型预测相关的特征，但无法清晰解释为何模型会做出特定的决策，缺乏对决策推理过程的深入理解。

Method: 该方法首先使用因果发现方法识别变量间的因果关系，随后将其转化为双极论辩框架（BAF）来表示特征间的支持与对立关系，最后通过半稳定语义寻找能够解释特定决策结果的特征扩展集。

Result: 在两个基准数据集上进行了方法验证，并与标准事后可解释性方法进行了对比分析，展示了该方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过整合因果性与论辩推理，该方法能够提供比传统特征重要性分析更深入的决策解释，有助于理解模型决策背后的推理机制。

Abstract: Explainable AI (XAI) methods identify which features are relevant to a model's predictions but often fail to clarify why certain decisions are made. In this work, we present a novel method that integrates causality with argument-based reasoning to explain why models may be making predictions. Our approach first identifies causal relationships among variables using causal discovery methods and then translates these into a Bipolar Argumentation Framework (BAF) to represent supportive and opposing interactions among features. By using semi-stable semantics, we find extensions of features that explain why certain outcomes may have been chosen. We demonstrate our method on two benchmark datasets and compare its results against standard post-hoc explainability approaches.

</details>


### [36] [LCGuard: Latent Communication Guard for Safe KV Sharing in Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22786)
*Sadia Asif,Mohammad Mohammadi Amiri,Momin Abbas,Prasanna Sattigeri,Karthikeyan Natesan Ramamurthy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出了LCGuard框架，用于保护基于KV缓存的大语言模型多智能体系统中的潜在通信安全，防止敏感信息通过共享缓存泄露，同时保持任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于大语言模型的多智能体系统越来越多地使用潜在通信（特别是通过transformer键值缓存KV caches）来协调复杂任务。然而，KV缓存编码了上下文输入、中间推理状态和智能体特定信息，创建了一个不透明的通道，敏感内容可能在没有明确文本披露的情况下跨智能体传播，存在隐私泄露风险。

Method: 提出了LCGuard（Latent Communication Guard）框架，将共享KV缓存视为潜在工作记忆，在缓存制品跨智能体传输之前学习表示级变换。通过重建方法形式化表示级敏感信息泄露：如果对抗性解码器能从共享缓存制品中恢复智能体特定的敏感输入，则该缓存制品是不安全的。采用对抗训练方法，其中对抗方学习重建敏感输入，而LCGuard学习保持任务相关语义并减少可重建信息的变换。

Result: 在多个模型族和多智能体基准测试上的实证评估表明，与标准KV共享基线相比，LCGuard能够持续降低基于重建的信息泄露和攻击成功率，同时保持具有竞争力的任务性能。

Conclusion: LCGuard框架成功解决了基于KV缓存的潜在通信中的敏感信息泄露问题，为多智能体LLM系统中的安全通信提供了有效解决方案，在隐私保护和任务性能之间取得了良好平衡。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based multi-agent systems increasingly rely on intermediate communication to coordinate complex tasks. While most existing systems communicate through natural language, recent work shows that latent communication, particularly through transformer key-value (KV) caches, can improve efficiency and preserve richer task-relevant information. However, KV caches also encode contextual inputs, intermediate reasoning states, and agent-specific information, creating an opaque channel through which sensitive content may propagate across agents without explicit textual disclosure. To address this, we introduce \textbf{LCGuard} (Latent Communication Guard), a framework for safe KV-based latent communication in multi-agent LLM systems. LCGuard treats shared KV caches as latent working memory and learns representation-level transformations before cache artifacts are transmitted across agents. We formalize representation-level sensitive information leakage operationally through reconstruction: a shared cache artifact is unsafe if an adversarial decoder can recover agent-specific sensitive inputs from it. This leads to an adversarial training formulation in which the adversary learns to reconstruct sensitive inputs, while LCGuard learns transformations that preserve task-relevant semantics and reduce reconstructable information. Empirical evaluations across multiple model families and multi-agent benchmarks show that LCGuard consistently reduces reconstruction-based leakage and attack success rates while maintaining competitive task performance compared to standard KV-sharing baselines.

</details>


### [37] [What Counts as AI Sycophancy? A Taxonomy and Expert Survey of a Fragmented Construct](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21778)
*Meryl Ye,Lujain Ibrahim,Jessica Y. Bo,Myra Cheng,Ida Mattsson,Daniel Vennemeyer,Robert Kraut,Steve Rathje*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过文献综述和专家调查揭示了AI sycophancy（AI谄媚）概念定义不一致的问题，提出了一个分类法来区分不同类型的行为，并发现专家虽然普遍认为这是个问题，但在具体行为界定上存在分歧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI sycophancy研究存在术语使用不一致的问题，不同研究者、公司和政策制定者用同一术语描述不同行为，导致评估结果难以比较、缓解策略无法迁移、治理措施难以有效实施，因此需要建立统一的概念框架。

Method: 1) 系统回顾了70篇关于AI sycophancy的论文，开发了一个分类法，从两个维度对行为进行分类：一是针对用户的立场信念vs个人特质情感，二是通过显性直接语言vs隐性微妙行为（如框架、遗漏、语调）；2) 调研了106位AI sycophancy及相关领域的专家，考察研究者对哪些模型行为构成谄媚行为是否存在共识。

Result: 文献综述发现现有研究主要聚焦于针对用户信念的显性谄媚行为，而更微妙的、针对个人特质的行为相对缺乏研究；专家调查发现94.3%的专家认为谄媚是当前AI系统的重大问题，但专家们在具体哪些行为算作谄媚行为上存在显著分歧。

Conclusion: AI sycophancy是一个包含多种行为的广泛家族，不同行为有不同的测量挑战、干预要求和治理影响；本文提出的分类法为理解和处理这些行为提供了共享词汇表，有助于促进未来研究、评估和治理工作的协调统一。

Abstract: AI sycophancy has become a prominent concern in large language model (LLM) research. Yet the term lacks a consistent definition and has been applied to behaviors ranging from agreeing with a user's false claim to excessively praising the user to withholding corrective feedback. When researchers, companies, and policymakers use the same term to describe different behaviors, evaluation results become difficult to compare, mitigation strategies fail to transfer, and systems that are resistant to one form of sycophancy continue exhibiting other forms. To address this, we make two contributions. First, we reviewed 70 papers on AI sycophancy to develop a taxonomy of how the behavior has been defined and measured. The taxonomy distinguishes (1) whether a model is sycophantic toward a user's positions and beliefs, or toward the user's broader personal traits and emotions, and (2) whether this occurs through explicit, direct language or more implicit, subtle behaviors such as framing, omission, or tone. Mapping existing literature to our taxonomy reveals that current research has focused on overt forms of sycophancy toward users' beliefs, leaving more subtle and person-directed behaviors relatively understudied. Second, we surveyed 106 experts in AI sycophancy and related fields to examine whether researchers agree on which model behaviors are sycophantic. While experts are nearly unanimous in believing that sycophancy is a significant problem in current AI systems (94.3% agree), they disagree substantially on which specific behaviors qualify. Together, these findings demonstrate that AI sycophancy is a broad family of behaviors with different measurement challenges, intervention requirements, and governance implications. Our taxonomy provides a shared vocabulary for understanding and addressing these behaviors.

</details>


### [38] [Implicit Safety Alignment from Crowd Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21822)
*Qian Lin,Daniel S. Brown*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Safe Crowd Preference-based RL框架，通过从众包偏好数据中提取共享的安全标准，并将其迁移到下游强化学习任务中，在没有显式安全奖励的情况下实现了与oracle方法相当的安全性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLHF能够揭示超越任务完成的隐式目标（如安全考虑）。众包偏好数据集中不同用户可能表达不同的偏好或目标，但遵循相似的安全原则。现有直接奖励组合方法（优化偏好学习的奖励模型与下游任务奖励）存在固有局限性。需要从众包偏好中发现共享的安全标准，并将其迁移到下游任务以规范智能体行为并确保安全。

Method: 提出Safe Crowd Preference-based RL分层框架：1）从众包偏好中提取安全对齐的技能(safety-aligned skills)；2）通过高层策略组合这些技能以安全地解决下游任务。该框架避免了直接奖励组合的局限性，采用分层结构实现安全约束的迁移。

Result: 在安全强化学习环境和包含多样化用户目标与共享安全约束的LLM风格任务上的实验表明：该方法在没有显式安全奖励的情况下，显著降低了安全成本，同时实现了与使用真实安全信号训练的oracle方法相当的任务性能。

Conclusion: 所提出的分层框架能够有效地从众包偏好中提取共享的安全标准，并成功迁移到下游任务，在无需显式安全奖励的情况下实现了安全且高效的策略学习，为从人类偏好中学习隐式安全约束提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF) can reveal implicit objectives such as safety considerations that go beyond task completion. In this work, we focus on the common safety criteria embedded in crowd preference datasets, where different users may express distinct preferences or objectives, yet follow similar safety principles. Our aim is to discover shared safety criteria from crowd preferences and then transfer them to downstream RL tasks to regularize agent behavior and enforce safety. We first show that direct reward combination-optimizing a preference-learned reward model together with downstream task rewards-has inherent limitations. Motivated by this, we propose Safe Crowd Preference-based RL, a hierarchical framework that extracts safety-aligned skills from crowd preferences and composes them via a high-level policy to safely solve downstream tasks. Experiments across safe RL environments and a preliminary LLM-style task with diverse user goals and shared safety constraints demonstrate that our approach substantially lowers safety costs without access to explicit safety rewards, while achieving task performance comparable to oracle methods trained with ground-truth safety signals.

</details>


### [39] [Toward AI VIS Co-Scientists: A General and End-to-End Agent Harness for Solving Complex Data Visualization Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21825)
*Haichao Miao,Zhimin Li,Kuangshi Ai,Kaiyuan Tang,Chaoli Wang,Peer-Timo Bremer,Shusen Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个端到端的智能代理框架（VIS co-scientist），能够仅基于数据和高层任务描述，自主设计并生成定制化的可视化分析应用程序，在IEEE SciVis竞赛中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学研究中的数据检查、解释和交流至关重要，但通常需要数据管理、分析、可视化设计等多方面的专业知识，这对领域专家而言是重大挑战。许多研究者期望构建一个能够基于高层指令自主执行长期任务的通用AI co-scientist系统，本研究正是朝向这一愿景的重要探索。

Method: 提出了一个VIS co-scientist智能体框架，包含多个协调工作的代理和专业技能模块。系统能够自主进行探索性分析、规划、环境配置、实现、界面验证以及任务完成度评估。每个阶段生成文档和指令工件，指导后续工作并支持迭代优化。该框架仅需数据和高层任务描述即可工作。

Result: 在跨多个科学工程领域的IEEE SciVis竞赛中验证了该方法。竞赛具有真实世界的复杂性：模糊需求、多样数据模态、设计权衡和任务驱动验证。系统能够仅给定数据和目标任务，自主生成具有验证过的链接视图行为的功能性单页可视化应用，高度定制化以满足领域专家的具体任务需求。

Conclusion: 本研究成功实现了基于数据和高层描述自主设计可视化分析应用的目标，为构建能够自主分析数据并设计可视化解决方案的通用AI co-scientist提供了关键组件，代表了向通用AI科学家愿景迈出的重要一步。

Abstract: The ability to inspect, interpret, and communicate complex data is crucial for virtually any scientific endeavor, but often requires significant expertise outside the core domain ranging from data management and analysis to visualization design and implementation. We present an end-to-end agentic harness that, based on only the data and a high level description of the tasks, independently designs custom visual analysis applications (VIS apps). This represents an important step towards a general AI co-scientist envisioned by many as an autonomous system that can autonomously execute long horizon tasks based on high-level directions. Our proposed VIS co-scientist is an essential component of this broader AI co-scientist vision: a harness that can autonomously analyze data and design visualization solutions using a collection of agents and specialized skills that coordinate exploratory analysis, plan, configure the environment, implement, validate the interface, and most importantly evaluate the overall task completion. Each stage produces document and instruction artifacts that guide downstream work and enable iterative refinement. We validate this approach on IEEE SciVis Contests spanning multiple science and engineering fields. These contests serve as ideal proving grounds because they encode real-world complexity: ambiguous requirements, diverse data modalities, design trade-offs, and task-driven validation. Given only the data and target tasks, our system autonomously produces functional single-page VIS Apps with verified linked-view behavior, highly customized to domain experts' specified tasks and needs.

</details>


### [40] [FLUID: From Ephemeral IDs to Multimodal Semantic Codes for Industrial-Scale Livestreaming Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21832)
*Xinhang Yuan,Zexi Huang,Anjia Cao,Xudong Lu,Zikai Wang,Penghao Zhou,Chang Liu,Wentao Guo,Qinglei Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FLUID是首个在生产级直播推荐排序器中完全移除候选项ID的框架，通过跨域多模态编码器和后期融合设计，解决了直播推荐中因生命周期短导致的ID冷启动问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统推荐系统依赖ID嵌入学习协同信号，但直播间通常仅存活数十分钟，导致ID嵌入始终处于冷启动状态，基于ID的排序模型无法有效泛化。

Method: FLUID框架包含：(1)跨域多模态编码器，在短视频和直播上联合训练，生成离散层次化编码(LUCID)；(2)后期融合、无ID设计，将切片级和房间级LUCID作为独立token注入；(3)在线增量训练下采用分阶段预热策略保持稳定性。

Result: 在服务超10亿全球用户的工业级直播推荐系统中部署，FLUID实现+0.55%有效观看时长、+2.05%冷启动房间浏览量、+0.05%活跃时长的显著线上提升。

Conclusion: FLUID成功证明了在生产级直播推荐中完全移除候选项ID的可行性，通过跨域多模态表示和层次化编码有效解决了直播推荐的ID冷启动挑战，为实时内容推荐提供了新范式。

Abstract: Modern recommender systems rely heavily on ID-based collaborative filtering: each item is represented by a unique ID embedding that accumulates collaborative signals from user interactions. Livestreaming recommendation, however, faces a unique challenge in this paradigm: a live room typically broadcasts for only tens of minutes, so its item ID remains poorly learned in a persistent cold-start state and ID-centric ranking models fail to generalize. We present FLUID, the first framework to fully retire the candidate-side item ID from a production-scale livestreaming ranker. FLUID couples a cross-domain multimodal encoder, jointly trained on short videos and livestreams to produce discrete hierarchical codes (LUCID), with a late-fusion, ID-free design that injects slice-level and room-level LUCID as independent tokens, stabilized by a staged warmup under online incremental training. Deployed on our industrial livestreaming recommenders with a cross-platform combined user base of over one billion globally, FLUID delivers significant online gains of +0.55% Quality Watch Duration, +2.05% Cold-Start Room Views, and +0.05% Active Hours.

</details>


### [41] [Planning in the LLM Era: Building for Reliability and Efficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21902)
*Michael Katz,Harsha Kokel,Kavitha Srinivas,Shirin Sohrabi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文论证了LLM规划领域正从“推理时生成计划”转向“生成可验证的符号规划器”，这一范式转移有助于构建更可靠、高效的智能体规划系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早期利用LLM进行规划的方法（单次生成或混合搜索）本质上是不可靠且不完备的，资源消耗大且泛化能力差，无法满足智能体对可靠性和资源效率的迫切需求。

Method: 对LLM时代的规划领域进行系统性综述，聚焦于“规划器生成”这一新兴范式，深入分析三类主流的规划器生成方法。

Result: 明确了当前规划器生成方法存在的主要局限性，并勾勒出实现更可靠、高效的LLM规划器生成所需的研究步骤与技术路径。

Conclusion: 这种范式转移反映了LLM时代规划领域的根本性重构，是构建可维护、低依赖LLM推理的可靠规划系统的关键方向。

Abstract: Growing attention to intelligent agents has put a spotlight on one of their central capabilities: planning. Early attempts to leverage large language models (LLMs) for planning relied on single-shot plan generation, followed by hybrid approaches that coupled LLMs with limited external search. These methods, unsound and incomplete by their very nature, often require substantial resources without yielding better solutions on unseen problems. As the limitations of LLMs become clearer, recent work has shifted toward using them at solution construction time -- generating symbolic solvers for a family of problems that can be verified and then used efficiently at inference time. This trend reflects the growing need for agents that are both reliable and resource-efficient. It also offers a path towards generating maintainable planners with minimal dependence on language models at inference time. In this paper, we argue that this shift reflects a broader realignment of the planning field in the LLM era. We examine three major categories of planner-generation methods, discuss their current limitations, and outline research steps towards a more reliable and efficient LLM-based generation of planners.

</details>


### [42] [Format-Constraint Coupling in Knowledge Graph Construction from Statistical Tables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21974)
*Jingxuan Qi,Zhiqiang Ye,Yuxiang Feng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在统计CSV数据上，提取模式可能降低知识图谱保真度。序列化格式与模式约束存在超叠加效应，格式-模式错配会导致灾难性失配，该现象称为"格式-约束耦合"。直接图访问能暴露高达+47.6pp的构建质量差异，而标准检索模式则掩盖了这些差异。发布了CSVFidelity-Bench基准测试集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究在开放数据门户常见的国家-年度时间序列矩阵CSV数据上，提取模式是否会降低知识图谱保真度，以及序列化格式与模式约束之间的相互作用机制。

Method: 采用2×2因子实验设计，在6个数据集上系统研究序列化格式和模式约束的独立效应与联合效应；通过探测和token消融实验验证表面形式锚定解释；跨不同格式-模式配对、GraphRAG主机和LLM家族进行控制变体实验。

Result: (1)格式与模式约束的联合效应超出独立效应之和高达+1.180（超叠加效应）；(2)在4/6数据集上95%置信区间严格为正，宽Type-II矩阵证据最强；(3)模式与错配格式会导致事实覆盖率低于无约束基线（4/6数据集），表现为实体膨胀或提取拒绝；(4)标准检索模式掩盖构建质量差异（delta≤1pp），而直接图访问暴露高达+47.6pp差异（p<0.0001）。

Conclusion: 发现了"格式-约束耦合"现象，其机制为基于列名引用的表面形式锚定；不同LLM家族表现存在差异；评估方式影响质量检测能力；发布CSVFidelity-Bench（含15数据集、1,892条黄金标准事实）支持保真度感知评估。

Abstract: An extraction schema should not reduce knowledge graph fidelity. On statistical CSV, however, it can. We study country-by-year time-series matrices, a common layout on open-data portals. In this setting, serialization format and schema constraints interact super-additively. Their joint effect exceeds the sum of independent effects by up to +1.180 (2x2 factorial, 6 datasets). Bootstrap 95% CIs are strictly positive on 4/6 datasets, with strongest evidence on wide Type-II matrices. More critically, a schema applied to a mismatched format can trigger catastrophic mismatch. Fact coverage falls below the unconstrained baseline on 4/6 datasets through entity inflation or extraction refusal. We call this observed pattern format-constraint coupling. Probing and token ablation support a surface-form anchoring explanation centred on column-name references. Controlled variants across format-schema pairings, GraphRAG hosts, and LLM families show the same direction within the measured scope; one LLM family shows only partial activation. The observation also has a diagnostic consequence. Three standard retrieval modes largely mask construction quality (delta <= 1pp), whereas direct graph access exposes gaps up to +47.6pp (p < 0.0001). To support fidelity-aware evaluation, we release CSVFidelity-Bench. It contains 15 datasets, 11 Type-II matrices, 4 Type-III tables, and 1,892 Gold Standard facts across 6 domains.

</details>


### [43] [Echo: Learning from Experience Data via User-Driven Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21984)
*Hande Dong,Xiaoyun Liang,Jiarui Yu,Jiayi Lin,Changqing Ai,Feng Liu,Wenjun Zhang,Rongbi Wei,Chaofan Zhu,Linjie Che,Feng Wu,Xin Shen,Dexu Kong,Xiaotian Wang,Qiuyuan Chen,Bingxu An,Yueting Lei,Qiang Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Echo框架，通过利用用户对AI代理输出的修正反馈作为高质量训练信号，将原始交互日志转化为可学习的知识，在代码补全场景中将接受率从25.7%提升至35.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静态人类数据存在扩展成本高且受限于创建者知识边界的固有缺陷。尽管从代理与环境交互的经验数据中持续学习有望突破这些限制，但原始交互日志充满噪声和试错过程，信息密度低，难以直接用于模型训练。

Method: 提出Echo框架，系统性地收集用户修正反馈作为高质量训练信号。当用户出于对结果负责而将错误的代理提案修正为验证过的解决方案时，这些修正序列将代理的粗略尝试蒸馏为高质量训练数据，Echo将这些信号系统地整合回训练循环中以持续优化模型。

Result: 在生产环境的大规模代码补全任务验证中，Echo成功突破了静态性能天花板，将代码建议的接受率从25.7%显著提升至35.7%。

Conclusion: Echo框架证明了通过系统性地利用用户修正反馈来持续学习是可行的，能够有效突破静态数据训练的性能瓶颈，实现模型与真实世界需求的持续对齐。

Abstract: Static "human data" faces inherent limitations: it is expensive to scale and bounded by the knowledge of its creators. Continuous learning from "experience data" - interactions between agents and their environments - promises to transcend these barriers. Today, the widespread deployment of AI agents grants us low-cost access to massive streams of such real-world experience. However, raw interaction logs are inherently noisy, filled with trial-and-error and low information density, rendering them inefficient for direct model training.
  We introduce Echo, a generalized framework designed to operationalize the transition from raw experience to learnable knowledge, effectively "echoing" environmental feedback back into the training loop for model optimization. In today's agent ecosystem, user refinement serves as a primary source of such feedback: driven by responsibility for the outcome, users rigorously transform flawed agent proposals into verified solutions. These user-driven refinement sequences inherently distill agents' crude attempts into high-quality training signals. Echo systematically harvests these signals to continuously align the agent with real-world needs. Large-scale validation in a production code completion environment confirms that Echo effectively harnesses this pipeline, breaking the static performance ceiling by increasing the acceptance rate from 25.7% to 35.7%.

</details>


### [44] [ECPO: Evidence-Coupled Policy Optimization for Evidence-Certified Candidate Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21993)
*Miaobo Hu,Shuhao Hu,BoKun Wang,Yina Sa,Xin Wang,Xiaobo Guo,Daren Zha,Jun Xiao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出证据认证的候选排序任务，要求系统输出Top-K列表及可恢复决策的文档跨度证据证书；引入ECPO方法，通过耦合排序效用、证书有效性和证据循环奖励三个奖励，优化联合排序与证据证书的策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 决策支持中的排序系统不仅要排序候选，还应提供可独立核查的证据；现有方法缺乏对证据证书的显式建模和约束，难以保证决策与证据的可追溯性和一致性。

Method: 提出Evidence-Coupled Policy Optimization (ECPO)：定义证据认证候选排序任务，学习可解释轨迹奖励（骨架对齐、论元一致性、图特征），优化包含列表排序效用、跨度证书有效性、证据循环奖励的约束策略，实现排序与证据证书的联合优化。

Result: 在MAVEN-ERE和RAMS数据集上，ECPO相比零样本、SFT、GRPO策略、奖励模型打分、约束解码、验证重试、最佳选择及事后证据理性化方法，在CertNDCG和决策-证据耦合指标上表现更优。

Conclusion: ECPO有效实现了排序决策与证据证书的耦合优化，提升了决策可解释性和可审计性，为决策支持系统提供了新的建模范式。

Abstract: Ranking systems used in decision-support settings should not only order candidates but also expose evidence that can be independently checked. We study evidence-certified candidate ranking: given an intent_id, a predefined plan skeleton, a window-local candidate roster, and text-derived candidate trajectories with span provenance, a system must output a Top-K list together with doc_id:span evidence certificates whose cited spans are sufficient to recover the decision. We instantiate this task on MAVEN-ERE and RAMS with fixed upstream extraction, window-local randomized candidate identifiers, skeleton-aligned trajectory supervision, hard negatives, and audit references. We introduce Evidence-Coupled Policy Optimization (ECPO), a listwise policy-optimization objective whose action is the joint object of ranking and evidence certificate. ECPO first learns an interpretable trajectory reward from skeleton alignment, argument consistency, and optional graph features; it then optimizes a constrained policy with three coupled rewards: listwise ranking utility, span-level certificate validity, and an evidence-cycle reward computed by a label-free deterministic verifier that reconstructs candidate support from claim-stripped cited spans. This reframes the goal from maximizing ordinary NDCG alone to maximizing CertNDCG and decision-evidence coupling. The evaluation compares ECPO against zero-shot, SFT, and GRPO policies, RM-only scoring with deterministic evidence attachment, grammar/JSON-constrained decoding, validator retry, best-of-N RM selection, and post-hoc evidence rationalization under closed-roster, predicted-roster, and hybrid-roster settings.

</details>


### [45] [Active Evidence-Seeking and Diagnostic Reasoning in Large Language Models for Clinical Decision Support](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22047)
*Chen Zhan,Xihe Qiu,Xiaoyu Tan,Xibing Zhuang,Gengchen Ma,Yue Zhang,Shuo Li,Peifeng Liu,Xiaoxiao Ge,Liang Liu,Lu Gan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究引入OSCE标准化病人模拟器进行交互式诊断评估，发现相比静态全上下文评估，多轮证据收集使诊断准确率下降12.75%，证据质量下降24.36%，表明静态基准可能高估模型在临床互动场景中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在静态医学考试中表现优异，但临床诊断常需在不确定性下进行迭代式证据收集。现有评估方法缺乏对主动诊断询问能力的考察，需建立更贴近真实临床场景的交互式评估框架。

Method: 构建OSCE启发的标准化病人模拟器，建立可控可复现的主动诊断询问基准测试。在468个病例上对15个模型进行协议测试，对比多轮证据收集与全上下文评估的性能差异，并进行错误分析。

Result: 多轮证据收集使诊断准确率降低12.75%，支持证据质量降低24.36%。错误分析显示性能下降与过早诊断闭合和低效提问策略相关。静态全上下文基准测试高估了交互式证据收集场景下的性能。

Conclusion: 静态全上下文基准可能高估模型在交互式证据收集场景中的表现，需补充交互式评估方法以确保临床决策支持系统的安全性。

Abstract: Large language models perform well on static medical examinations, yet clinical diagnosis often requires iterative evidence gathering under uncertainty. Building on prior interactive evaluation efforts, we introduce an OSCE-inspired standardized patient simulator and a controlled, reproducible benchmark for active diagnostic inquiry. Across 468 cases and 15 models in our protocol, we observe that multi-turn evidence seeking reduces diagnostic accuracy by 12.75% and lowers supporting-evidence quality by 24.36% relative to full-context evaluation; error analyses associate these drops with premature diagnostic closure and inefficient questioning. Together, these results suggest that static full-context benchmarks may overestimate performance in interactive evidence-seeking settings, motivating complementary interactive assessment for safer clinical decision support.

</details>


### [46] [Enhancing Visual Token Representations for Video Large Language Models via Training-Free Spatial-Temporal Pooling and Gridding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22078)
*Bingjun Luo,Tony Wang,Hanqi Chen,Xinpeng Ding*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为ST-GridPool的免训练视觉token增强方法，通过金字塔时序网格化(PTG)和基于范数的空间池化(NSP)来提升视频大语言模型的性能，无需重新训练即可即插即用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态大语言模型(如LLaVA系列)在视频理解任务中，采用简单的池化或插值技术压缩视觉token，忽略了视觉token之间复杂的时空动态交互，导致在压缩效率和时空交互保持之间存在不足。

Method: 提出ST-GridPool方法，包含两个核心组件：(1)金字塔时序网格化(PTG)，通过分层时序网格化捕获多粒度的时空交互；(2)基于范数的空间池化(NSP)，利用token范数与语义丰富度的相关性来保留高信息量的视觉区域。该方法无需训练，可直接集成到现有Video LLMs中。

Result: 在多个基准测试上进行的大量实验表明，ST-GridPool能够持续提升视频大语言模型的性能，证明了该方法的有效性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: ST-GridPool为改进视觉token表示提供了一种高效、即插即用的解决方案，无需昂贵的重新训练成本，为视频理解任务中的视觉token压缩与增强提供了新思路。

Abstract: Recent advances in Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have significantly advanced video understanding tasks, yet challenges remain in efficiently compressing visual tokens while preserving spatiotemporal interactions. Existing methods, such as LLaVA family, utilize simplistic pooling or interpolation techniques that overlook the intricate dynamics of visual tokens. To bridge this gap, we propose ST-GridPool, a novel training-free visual token enhancement method designed specifically for Video LLMs. Our approach integrates Pyramid Temporal Gridding (PTG), which captures multi-grained spatiotemporal interactions through hierarchical temporal gridding, and Norm-based Spatial Pooling (NSP), which preserves high-information visual regions by leveraging the correlation between token norms and semantic richness. Extensive experiments on various benchmarks demonstrate that ST-GridPool consistently enhances performance of Video LLMs without requiring costly retraining. Our method offers an efficient and plug-and-play solution for improving visual token representations. Our code is available in https://github.com/bingjunluo/ST-GridPool.

</details>


### [47] [A Camera-Cooperative ISAC Framework for Multimodal Non-Cooperative UAVs Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22090)
*Wenfeng Wu,Luping Xiang,Kun Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了CC-ISAC框架，通过相机与ISAC的多模态协同感知实现高效无人机波束引导与跟踪，显著降低了系统开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非合作无人机的检测对ISAC系统构成重大挑战，主要源于单模态感知的固有局限性以及通信与感知资源对共享资源的竞争。

Method: 提出Camera-Cooperative ISAC (CC-ISAC)框架，采用相机进行粗粒度空域监测，ISAC进行细粒度高精度感知，形成互补感知环路；开发了两个关键模块：(1)视觉-回波数据对齐(V2EDA)模型，通过交叉注意力机制对齐视觉和回波域特征；(2)多模态融合估计(MMFE)模型，整合历史多模态数据与当前观测进行鲁棒状态估计。

Result: 在DeepSense 6G数据集上的广泛评估表明，该框架实现了波束引导开销平均降低71%，跟踪开销降低1.69-11.15%，同时保持高角度估计精度。

Conclusion: CC-ISAC框架有效缓解了感知与通信间的资源竞争，实现了可靠的无人机监视，同时释放了大量系统资源用于额外通信任务，代表了ISAC系统设计的实际进步。

Abstract: The detection of non-cooperative unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) presents significant challenges for Integrated Sensing and Communication (ISAC) systems due to the inherent limitations of single-modal perception and the competition for shared communication and sensing resources. To address these challenges, this paper proposes a novel Camera-Cooperative ISAC (CC-ISAC) framework that employs multimodal sensing to enable efficient UAV beam steering and tracking. The proposed framework employs cameras for coarse-grained airspace monitoring and utilizes ISAC for fine-grained, high-precision sensing, forming a complementary perception loop that enhances both sensing accuracy and resource efficiency. Within this framework, two key modules are developed: (1) a Vision-to-Echo Data Alignment (V2EDA) model that aligns visual and echo-domain features through cross-attention mechanisms, and (2) a Multimodal Fusion-Based Estimation (MMFE) model that integrates historical multimodal data with current observations for robust state estimation. Extensive evaluations conducted on the DeepSense 6G dataset demonstrate that the proposed framework achieves an average reduction of 71% in beam steering overhead and 1.69-11.15% in tracking overhead while maintaining high angular estimation accuracy. The CC-ISAC framework effectively mitigates resource contention between sensing and communication, enabling reliable UAV surveillance while freeing substantial system resources for additional communication tasks, thereby representing a practical advancement in ISAC system design.

</details>


### [48] [Knowledge Graph Re-engineering Along the Ontological Continuum (extended version)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22093)
*Enrico Daga,Valentina Tamma,Terry Payne*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了“本体连续体”概念，作为描述和转换知识图谱的理论框架，旨在解决知识图谱建模实践多样性带来的集成与重用难题，特别适用于神经符号AI与生成式AI的自动化需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识图谱已成为数据集成的主要载体，但其建模实践的多样性（从轻量级词汇到丰富公理化的本体）使得集成和重用成本高且脆弱。这一挑战在神经符号AI中尤为突出，因为桥接神经与符号组件依赖于对知识图谱的重构以适应新需求；尽管生成式AI提供了前所未有的自动化能力，但缺乏对知识图谱空间的系统性理解，使这种自动化缺乏理论基础。

Method: 提出了“本体连续体”这一理论构念，其特征框架由两个正交区分定义：语义vs语用，以及属性vs可供性。采用实证方法论立场，不规定知识图谱应如何建模，而是通过观察真实世界的知识图谱工程实践，定义现存知识图谱的理论，并通过形式概念分析（FCA）等方法使其结构形式化明确。通过溯源知识的案例研究来验证该框架。

Result: 建立了一个描述、比较、导航和转换知识图谱的全范围建模实践的词汇表和概念框架；通过溯源知识的案例研究，展示了单一关注点如何在本体连续体中表现出不同的形态；识别并阐述了五个开放性研究挑战。

Conclusion: 本体连续体为理解知识图谱建模实践空间提供了缺失的概念化基础，能够支持神经符号AI和生成式AI的知识图谱自动化重构需求。该方法论不强制规范建模方式，而是基于实证观察发展理论，邀请社区共同开发本体连续体作为共享的研究议程。

Abstract: Knowledge graphs have become the primary vehicle for data integration and are critical to the success of modern AI, but the diversity of KG modelling practices, from lightweight vocabularies to richly axiomatised ontologies, makes integration and reuse expensive and brittle. This challenge is particularly acute in neuro-symbolic AI, where bridging neural and symbolic components depends on the ability to reengineer KGs to fit new requirements; GenAI now offers unprecedented automation capability, but without a principled understanding of the KG space, such automation remains conceptually ungrounded. We introduce the ontological continuum as that missing conceptualisation, a theoretical construct a theoretical construct whose characterisation framework is defined by two orthogonal distinctions: semantics vs pragmatics, and properties vs affordances; together these define a vocabulary to describe, compare, navigate, and transform KGs across the full range of modelling practices. The methodological stance is empirical: rather than prescribing how KGs should be modelled, the continuum aims to define a theory of the existent, derived from observation of real-world KG engineering practices and whose structure can be made formally explicit, for example, through Formal Concept Analysis (FCA). We ground the vision through a case study on provenance knowledge, showing how a single concern manifests differently across the continuum. We articulate five open research challenges and invite the community to develop the ontological continuum as a shared research agenda.

</details>


### [49] [MPDocBench-Parse: Benchmarking Practical Multi-page Document Parsing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22100)
*Bangbang Zhou,Hangdi Xing,Yifan Chen,Jianjun Xu,Qi Zheng,Feiyu Gao,Zhibo Yang,Shuai Bai,Ming Yan,Jieping Ye,Hongtao Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了MPDocBench-Parse，一个面向真实场景的多页文档解析基准，包含433份文档、3246页，覆盖中英文15种文档类型，支持细粒度评估语义连续性、层级结构恢复和视觉内容保留。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文档解析基准存在明显不足：要么专注于特定任务，要么仅评估单页、以文本为中心的场景，无法满足实际多页解析需求；同时缺乏对语义连续性、层级结构恢复和视觉内容保留的细粒度评估。

Method: 构建了包含433份人工标注文档（3246页）的MPDocBench-Parse基准，覆盖中英文15种文档类型和多样布局风格；设计了全面的文档级端到端评估协议，涵盖内容保真度和逻辑结构，包括文本、表格、公式识别，跨页文本表格合并，图像提取，阅读顺序和标题层级恢复。

Result: 实验表明，现有模型在基础文本提取上表现良好，但在语义连续性整合、视觉内容解析和层级结构恢复方面仍存在明显局限性。

Conclusion: MPDocBench-Parse为推动文档解析向更真实场景发展提供了统一的评估基础，揭示了当前模型在复杂文档解析任务中的不足，为未来研究指明了方向。

Abstract: Document parsing converts visually rich documents into machine-readable structured representations, forming a crucial foundation for information systems. Although many benchmarks have been proposed for document parsing, they remain inadequate for realistic scenarios. Existing benchmarks either focus on specific tasks or assess only single-page, text-centric settings, making them insufficient for practical multi-page parsing. Moreover, they lack fine-grained evaluation of semantic continuity, hierarchical structure recovery, and visual content preservation. To address these gaps, we propose MPDocBench-Parse, a benchmark for multi-page document parsing in real-world applications. It contains 433 manually annotated documents with 3,246 pages, covering 15 document types in English and Chinese, with diverse layout styles, and supports document-level end-to-end evaluation. We further design a comprehensive protocol for content fidelity and logical structure, covering text, table, and formula recognition, truncated text and table merging, figure extraction, reading order, and heading hierarchy recovery. Experiments show that, while existing models perform well on basic text extraction, they still suffer clear limitations in semantic continuity integration, visual content parsing, and hierarchical structure recovery. MPDocBench-Parse provides a unified foundation for advancing document parsing toward more realistic scenarios.

</details>


### [50] [ExComm: Exploration-Stage Communication for Error-Resilient Agentic Test-Time Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22102)
*Woomin Song,Beomjun Kim,Daewon Choi,Sai Muralidhar Jayanthi,Saket Dingliwal,Jinwoo Shin,Aram Galstyan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ExComm通信协议，通过检测和解决多智能体推理中的事实冲突来缓解长周期任务中的错误传播问题，在AIME和GAIA基准测试上平均性能提升5.7%和5.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试时扩展方法在长周期智能体任务中存在错误传播问题：中间步骤引入的事实错误或无效推演会持续存在于智能体的信念状态中并污染后续推理。现有方法依赖智能体自我检测错误、在错误轨迹中选择或仅在错误已影响推理路径后才进行修正，控制能力有限。作者观察到并行智能体推理中的大多数中间错误会产生可检测的跨智能体事实冲突，这为主动纠错提供了机会。

Method: 提出ExComm（Exploration-stage Communication Protocol），包含三个核心组件：1）周期性审核智能体信念状态以检测跨智能体事实冲突；2）通过基于工具的验证循环解决冲突，并向相关智能体返回针对性的简明反馈；3）采用软信念更新机制，将验证后的反馈附加而非覆盖现有信念；4）引入轨迹多样化模块，将冗余轨迹重定向至正交策略，防止通信导致轨迹多样性坍塌。该方法利用智能体工作流的迭代结构，在探索阶段实现主动纠错。

Result: 在AIME 2024、AIME 2025和GAIA基准测试上，使用Gemini-2.5-Flash-Lite和Qwen3.5-4B模型，ExComm相比最佳基线方法分别获得5.7%和5.0%的平均性能提升。分析显示：改进的错误恢复能力、良好的扩展性能、比适配的通信基线更强的多样性，以及在评估方法中最优的性能-成本权衡。

Conclusion: ExComm通过利用并行智能体推理中自然产生的事实冲突作为纠错信号，有效缓解了长周期智能体任务中的错误传播问题，在保持轨迹多样性的同时实现了显著的性能提升，为测试时扩展方法提供了更有效的误差控制机制。

Abstract: A common failure mode in long-horizon agentic test-time scaling is error propagation, where factual errors or invalid deductions introduced at intermediate steps persist in the agent's belief state and contaminate later reasoning. Existing test-time scaling methods provide limited control over this process, as they often rely on agents to detect their own mistakes, select among flawed trajectories, or refine solutions only after errors have already shaped the reasoning path. We propose ExComm, a communication protocol for exploration-stage agentic test-time scaling. ExComm is motivated by the empirical observation that the majority of intermediate errors in parallel agentic reasoning produce detectable cross-agent factual conflicts. Leveraging the iterative structure of agentic workflows, ExComm periodically audits agent belief states to detect such conflicts, resolves them through a dedicated tool-based verification loop, and returns concise, targeted feedback to the involved agents. Corrections are incorporated through soft belief updates, which append verified feedback rather than overwriting existing beliefs. Furthermore, to prevent collapsing trajectory diversity due to communication, ExComm further introduces a trajectory diversification module that redirects redundant trajectories toward orthogonal strategies. Experiments on AIME 2024, AIME 2025, and GAIA with Gemini-2.5-Flash-Lite and Qwen3.5-4B show that ExComm consistently outperforms strong test-time scaling baselines, achieving average performance gains of 5.7% and 5.0% over the best-performing baselines, respectively. Further analyses demonstrate improved error recovery, favorable scaling behavior, stronger diversity than adapted communication baselines, and the best performance-cost trade-off among the evaluated methods.

</details>


### [51] [ArborKV: Structure-Aware KV Cache Management for Scaling Tree-based LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22106)
*Yeqiu Chen,Ziyan Liu,Zhenxin Huang,Runquan Gui,Hong Wang,Lei Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ArborKV，一种面向树形推理的结构感知KV缓存淘汰框架，在保持精度的同时实现约4倍的峰值内存降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 树形思维（ToT）等显式搜索推理方法需要维护大量中间推理状态的KV缓存，导致内存成为严重瓶颈，限制了搜索深度和宽度；而现有KV缓存在此类场景下缺乏高效的复用与淘汰机制。

Method: 提出ArborKV框架：结合轻量级价值估计器与树感知分配策略，对活跃分支及其祖先给予高优先级保留，对不活跃子树进行纯token提取式淘汰；采用惰性重水化机制支持回溯时的状态恢复，从而实现结构感知的内存管理。

Result: 在ToT推理基准测试中，ArborKV实现最高约4倍的峰值KV内存减少，同时保持近乎全保留精度，使在固定硬件预算下可支持原本会内存溢出的更大规模搜索配置。

Conclusion: ArborKV有效缓解了ToT风格推理的KV缓存内存瓶颈，在几乎不损失准确率的前提下大幅提升内存效率，为更复杂的树形搜索推理提供了实用的内存优化方案。

Abstract: Recent progress in LLM reasoning has increasingly shifted from single-pass generation to explicit search over intermediate reasoning states. Tree-of-Thoughts (ToT) organizes inference to tree-structured search with branching and backtracking, but it substantially amplifies the Key--Value (KV) cache: retaining KV states for a frontier of partial trajectories quickly becomes a memory bottleneck that limits throughput and constrains search depth and width under fixed hardware budgets. We address this challenge by observing that KV reuse in ToT-style inference is governed by search dynamics: near-term decoding depends primarily on the active branch and its ancestors, whereas inactive subtrees have low short-term reuse probability yet must remain recoverable for backtracking. Motivated by this, we propose ArborKV, a structure-aware eviction framework that couples a lightweight value estimator with a tree-aware allocation policy, and performs purely token-extractive eviction with lazy rehydration to support revisits. Experiments on ToT-style reasoning benchmarks show that ArborKV achieves up to ~4x peak KV-memory reduction while preserving near-full-retention accuracy, enabling larger search configurations under fixed device budgets that would otherwise run out of memory.

</details>

*Caixin Kang,Tianyu Yan,Sitong Gong,Mingfang Zhang,Liangyang Ouyang,Ruicong Liu,Bo Zheng,Huchuan Lu,Kaipeng Zhang,Yoichi Sato,Yifei Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Grounded Personality Reasoning (GPR)任务和MM-OCEAN数据集，通过三层评估框架发现当前多模态大语言模型存在严重的"偏见差距"：51%的正确评分缺乏证据支撑，暴露出模型在人格感知中"得分正确但推理错误"的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM人格感知评估基准仅关注大五人格分数预测的数值准确性，无法判断模型是否真正通过行为理解进行人格感知，还是仅通过表面模式匹配进行预判。需要建立证据锚定机制以区分真实理解与表面匹配。

Method: 提出三项贡献：(i)形式化GPR任务，要求MLLM通过"评分-推理-锚定"链条将大五人格评分锚定于可观察证据；(ii)构建MM-OCEAN数据集，包含1,104个视频和5,320个多选题，提供时间戳行为观察、证据锚定的特质分析及七类线索锚定问题；(iii)设计三层评估框架（评分、推理、锚定）和四种样本级失败模式指标：偏见率(PR)、臆造率(CR)、整合失败率(IR)和整体锚定率(HR)，对27个MLLM进行基准测试。

Result: 在27个模型（13个闭源、14个开源）上的基准测试揭示了显著的"偏见差距"：全领域中51%的正确评分未锚定于检索线索，整体锚定率仅达0-33.5%。这表明模型获得正确分数与进行正确推理之间存在断裂。

Conclusion: 研究发现MLLM在人格感知中存在"得分正确但推理错误"的脱节现象，为MLLM的锚定社会认知研究指明了发展路线图，强调需要发展真正基于证据的人格推理能力而非表面模式匹配。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) are increasingly deployed in human-facing roles where personality perception is critical, yet existing benchmarks evaluate this capability solely on numerical Big Five score prediction, leaving open whether models truly perceive personality through behavioral understanding or merely prejudge through superficial pattern matching. We address this gap with three contributions. (i) A new task: we formalize Grounded Personality Reasoning (GPR), which requires MLLMs to anchor each Big Five rating in observable evidence through a chain of rating, reasoning, and grounding. (ii) A new dataset: we release MM-OCEAN (1,104 videos, 5,320 MCQs), produced by a multi-agent pipeline with human verification, with timestamped behavioral observations, evidence-grounded trait analyses, and seven categories of cue-grounding MCQs. (iii) Benchmark and analysis: we design a three-tier evaluation (rating, reasoning, grounding) plus four sample-level failure-mode metrics: Prejudice Rate (PR), Confabulation Rate (CR), Integration-failure Rate (IR), and Holistic-grounding Rate (HR), and benchmark 27 MLLMs (13 closed, 14 open). The analysis uncovers a striking Prejudice Gap: across the field, 51% of correct ratings are not grounded in retrieved cues, and the Holistic-Grounding Rate spans only 0-33.5%. These findings expose a disconnect between getting the right score and reasoning for the right reason, charting a roadmap for grounded social cognition in MLLMs.

</details>


### [53] [Efficient Agentic Reasoning Through Self-Regulated Simulative Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22138)
*Mingkai Deng,Jinyu Hou,Lara Sá Neves,Varad Pimpalkhute,Taylor W. Killian,Zhengzhong Liu,Eric P. Xing*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SR²AM框架，通过系统分解（模拟推理System II、自我调节System III、反应执行System I）实现高效智能体推理，在多个任务上以更小模型和更少token达到大模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端训练的反应式策略无法显式控制规划的存在性、结构或时域范围，导致推理长度急剧增加、token使用低效且准确率提升不可靠。作者认为高效智能体推理需要显式分解决策机制。

Method: 将决策分解为三个系统：模拟推理(System II)基于世界模型进行未来状态预测的规划；自我调节(System III)学习何时及多深入地规划；反应执行(System I)处理细粒度动作。实现SR²AM，在LLM思维链中区分两阶段，LLM作为世界模型。探索两种实例化：v0.1通过提示多模块系统记录决策；v1.0从预训练推理LLM轨迹重建结构化计划，经监督学习后强化学习训练。

Result: v0.1-8B和v1.0-30B在数学、科学、表格分析、网页信息搜索任务上Pass@1分别与120-355B和685B-1T参数系统竞争力相当；v1.0-30B推理token减少25.8-95.3%。强化学习使平均规划视野增加22.8%，规划频率仅增长2.0%，表明学会向更远规划而非更频繁规划。

Conclusion: 学习型自我调节实例化了一个可扩展原则：智能体应能治理自身学习与适应，该原则有望从规划扩展至更广泛的智能体治理范畴。

Abstract: How should an agent decide when and how to plan? A dominant approach builds agents as reactive policies with adaptive computation (e.g., chain-of-thought), trained end-to-end expecting planning to emerge implicitly. Without control over the presence, structure, or horizon of planning, these systems dramatically increase reasoning length, yielding inefficient token use without reliable accuracy gains. We argue efficient agentic reasoning benefits from decomposing decision-making into three systems: simulative reasoning (System II) grounding deliberation in future-state prediction via a world model; self-regulation (System III) deciding when and how deeply to plan via a learned configurator; and reactive execution (System I) handling fine-grained action. Simulative reasoning provides unified planning across diverse tasks without per-domain engineering, while self-regulation ensures the planner is invoked only when needed. To test this, we develop SR$^2$AM (Self-Regulated Simulative Reasoning Agentic LLM), realizing both as distinct stages within an LLM's chain-of-thought, with the LLM as world model. We explore two instantiations: recording decisions from a prompted multi-module system (v0.1) and reconstructing structured plans from traces of pretrained reasoning LLMs (v1.0), trained via supervised then reinforcement learning (RL). Across math, science, tabular analysis, and web information seeking, v0.1-8B and v1.0-30B achieve Pass@1 competitive with 120-355B and 685B-1T parameter systems respectively, while v1.0-30B uses 25.8-95.3% fewer reasoning tokens than comparable agentic LLMs. RL increases average planning horizon by 22.8% while planning frequency grows only 2.0%, showing it learns to plan further ahead rather than more often. More broadly, learned self-regulation instantiates a principle we expect to extend beyond planning to how agents govern their own learning and adaptation.

</details>


### [54] [Ratchet: A Minimal Hygiene Recipe for Self-Evolving LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22148)
*Xing Zhang,Yanwei Cui,Guanghui Wang,Ziyuan Li,Wei Qiu,Bing Zhu,Peiyang He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Ratchet框架，通过引入生命周期管理机制（包括淘汰、容量上限、元技能指导等），显著提升了LLM自演化技能库的效果，在MBPP+ hard-100上实现了+0.328的pass@1提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自演化技能库系统（如Voyager）存在关键瓶颈：LLM自主编写的技能相比无技能基线几乎无提升（+0.0pp），而人工筛选的技能提升显著（+16.2pp）。这表明问题不在于技能编写，而在于技能生命周期管理的缺失。

Method: 提出Ratchet框架，一个单智能体循环系统，使冻结的LLM能够编写、检索、管理和淘汰自然语言技能。引入四种卫生机制：结果驱动的淘汰、有界活跃容量上限、元技能编写指导、模式规范化。通过理论分析提出非发散命题，确保性能不会退化到无技能基线以下。

Result: 在MBPP+ hard-100数据集上，使用Claude Opus 4.7，Ratchet将held-out pass@1从基线0.258±0.047提升至后期窗口滚动均值0.584（峰值0.658±0.042），相比无技能对照组的提升为+0.328±0.018。在SWE-bench Verified上实现+0.22的峰值提升。消融实验（A1-A8）揭示最小可行方案：淘汰机制和元技能编写先验是核心，显式去重可被元技能本身替代。

Conclusion: 技能生命周期管理是LLM智能体技能库成功的关键。Ratchet通过有效的技能管理机制解决了技能库退化问题，证明了自动化的技能筛选和淘汰对性能提升至关重要。该框架具有良好的跨任务迁移能力。

Abstract: Self-evolving skill libraries, pioneered by Voyager, let frozen LLM agents accumulate reusable knowledge without weight updates, yet recent evaluation shows that LLM-authored skills deliver $+0.0$pp over no-skill baselines while human-curated ones deliver $+16.2$pp: the bottleneck is not skill authoring but lifecycle management. We introduce \textbf{Ratchet}, a single-agent loop in which a frozen LLM writes, retrieves, curates, and retires its own natural-language skills. Ratchet integrates four candidate hygiene mechanisms: outcome-driven retirement, a bounded active-cap, meta-skill authoring guidance, and pattern canonicalisation. On MBPP+ hard-100 with Claude Opus 4.7, Ratchet lifts held-out pass@1 from a $0.258 \pm 0.047$ baseline to a late-window rolling mean of $0.584$ (peak $0.658 \pm 0.042$) across 100 rounds and 3 seeds, a $+0.328 \pm 0.018$ rolling-mean gain where the no-skill control drifts at $+0.002 \pm 0.005$; the same recipe transfers to an agentic solver on SWE-bench Verified ($+0.22$ peak lift over 20 rounds). Eight ablations (A1--A8) reveal that the minimal working recipe is smaller than our design suggests: retirement and the meta-skill authoring prior are load-bearing, while explicit deduplication (canonicalisation, cover-guard) is subsumed by the meta-skill itself. A non-divergence proposition shows that bounded cap and retirement threshold together prevent expected performance from drifting below the no-skills floor.

</details>


### [55] [IdleSpec: Exploiting Idle Time via Speculative Planning for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22154)
*Daewon Choi,Kyunghyun Park,Woomin Song,Saket Dingliwal,Sai Muralidhar Jayanthi,Jinwoo Shin,Aram Galstyan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出IdleSpec，一种在智能体推理空闲时间生成候选计划以提升性能的方法，在多个基准上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大语言模型(LLM)的智能体在迭代工具调用和环境交互过程中会产生不可避免的空闲等待时间。现有工作要么将其视为不可避免的开销，要么提出受限的解决方案，忽视了不同工具调用间的计算预算差异和未来观测的不确定性，导致空闲时间利用不充分。

Method: IdleSpec是一种可扩展的通用推理方法，利用空闲时间迭代生成候选计划，并在观测可用时进行聚合以指导下一步推理。针对观测不确定性下的有效计划生成，该方法从学习到的分布中采样互补的起草策略（渐进式和恢复式），并通过后验反馈更新分布。

Result: 在GAIA和FRAMES基准测试中，使用Gemini-2.5-Flash模型的IdleSpec实现55.6%的平均准确率，比不使用空闲时间的基线方法提升5.1%。在涉及代码执行大量延迟的MLE-Bench上，IdleSpec在Any Medal rate上实现了高达9.1%的性能提升。

Conclusion: IdleSpec通过有效利用空闲时间显著提升了智能体在各种场景下的性能，证明了其在长时间任务中的泛化能力，为智能体推理优化提供了新思路。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based agents solve complex tasks by leveraging multi-step reasoning with iterative tool calls and environment interactions, which incur idle time while waiting for observations. Despite the prevalence of idle time in most agentic scenarios, existing works treat it as an unavoidable overhead or propose restricted solutions that overlook varying computational budgets across different tool calls and future observation uncertainty, thereby leading to suboptimal utilization of idle time. In this paper, we introduce IdleSpec, a scalable and generic inference approach that leverages idle-time computation to improve agent performance while minimizing latency overhead. Specifically, IdleSpec iteratively generates plan candidates during idle periods and, once observations become available, aggregates them to guide the next reasoning step. For effective plan generation under observation uncertainty, IdleSpec samples between complementary drafting strategies (i.e., progressive and recovery) from a learned distribution that is updated via posterior feedback. Our experiments demonstrate that IdleSpec significantly improves agent performance in various agentic scenarios by effectively utilizing idle time. In particular, on the GAIA and FRAMES, IdleSpec achieves 55.6% average accuracy with Gemini-2.5-Flash, surpassing the vanilla baseline without idle-time usage by 5.1%. Furthermore, for MLE-Bench, which involves substantial delay from code executions, IdleSpec achieves performance gains of up to 9.1% on the Any Medal rate, highlighting its generalizability to long-horizon tasks.

</details>


### [56] [ST-SimDiff: Balancing Spatiotemporal Similarity and Difference for Efficient Video Understanding with MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22158)
*Bingjun Luo,Tony Wang,Chaoqi Chen,Xinpeng Ding*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了ST-SimDiff，一个无需训练的框架，通过相似性选择压缩静态信息，通过时序差异选择捕捉关键动态变化，在显著降低计算成本的同时优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态大语言模型在处理长视频时面临巨大的计算开销，现有方法主要通过剪枝或合并token来减少冗余，但忽视了视频内容中的变化和转折点这一关键维度，且缺乏对时空关系的协同建模。

Method: 构建时空图来统一建模视觉token之间的复杂关联；采用并行双重选择策略：1)基于相似性的选择使用社区检测保留代表性token，压缩静态信息；2)基于时序差异的选择精确定位内容变化点，保留捕捉关键动态变化的token。

Result: 大量实验表明，该方法在大幅降低计算成本的同时，显著优于最先进的方法，能够以最少的token数量同时保留静态和动态内容。

Conclusion: ST-SimDiff成功地将相似性用于识别冗余、差异用于捕捉关键事件的理念付诸实践，为长视频处理提供了一个高效且无需训练的解决方案。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) face significant computational overhead when processing long videos due to the massive number of visual tokens required. To improve efficiency, existing methods primarily reduce redundancy by pruning or merging tokens based on importance or similarity. However, these approaches largely overlook a critical dimension of video content, i.e., changes and turning points, and they lack a collaborative model for spatio-temporal relationships. To address this, we propose a new perspective: similarity is for identifying redundancy, while difference is for capturing key events. Based on this, we designed a training-free framework named ST-SimDiff. We first construct a spatio-temporal graph from the visual tokens to uniformly model their complex associations. Subsequently, we employ a parallel dual-selection strategy: 1) similarity-based selection uses community detection to retain representative tokens, compressing static information; 2) temporal difference-based selection precisely locates content-changing points to preserve tokens that capture key dynamic shifts. This allows it to preserve both static and dynamic content with a minimal number of tokens. Extensive experiments show our method significantly outperforms state-of-the-art approaches while substantially reducing computational costs. Our code is available in https://github.com/bingjunluo/ST-SimDiff.

</details>


### [57] [Adapting the Interface, Not the Model: Runtime Harness Adaptation for Deterministic LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22166)
*Tianshi Xu,Huifeng Wen,Meng Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Life-Harness，一种生命周期感知的运行时框架，通过将交互失败转化为可复用的干预措施来改进冻结的LLM代理，在126个模型-环境设置中有116个得到了改进，平均相对提升88.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM代理适应方法主要关注更新模型参数，但在确定性、规则约束的领域中，许多失败源于模型-环境接口层面的不匹配。作者认为运行时框架作为模型与环境的中间层，对代理行为有重要影响，应该作为一种独立于模型参数更新的改进方向。

Method: 提出Life-Harness，一种生命周期感知的运行时框架：(1)从训练轨迹中学习，识别重复出现的交互失败；(2)将这些失败转化为跨环境契约、程序技能、动作实现和轨迹调节四个层面的可复用干预措施；(3)在评估阶段保持固定。该方法不改变模型权重或评估环境。

Result: 在τ-bench、τ²-bench和AgentBench的7个确定性环境中，Life-Harness在126个模型-环境设置中改进了116个，涵盖18个模型骨干，平均相对改进88.5%。仅从Qwen3-4B-Instruct轨迹演化得到的框架可迁移至其他17个模型，证明了其捕获的是可复用的环境侧结构而非模型特定行为。

Conclusion: 运行时接口适应可以作为一种独立于模型中心训练的补充方法，为LLM代理的改进提供了新方向。Life-Harness展示了在不改变模型权重的情况下，通过优化模型-环境接口即可显著提升代理性能的可行性。

Abstract: LLM agents are shaped not only by their language models, but also by the runtime harness that mediates observation, tool use, action execution, feedback interpretation, and trajectory control. While existing agent adaptation methods mainly update model parameters, many failures in deterministic, rule-governed domains stem from mismatches at the model--environment interface. We propose Life-Harness, a lifecycle-aware runtime harness that improves frozen LLM agents without changing model weights or evaluation environments. Life-Harness evolves from training trajectories by converting recurring interaction failures into reusable interventions across environment contracts, procedural skills, action realization, and trajectory regulation, and remains fixed during held-out evaluation. On seven deterministic environments from $τ$-bench, $τ^2$-bench, and AgentBench, Life-Harness improves 116 out of 126 model--environment settings across 18 model backbones, with an average relative improvement of 88.5%. Harnesses evolved only from Qwen3-4B-Instruct trajectories transfer to 17 other models, showing that Life-Harness captures reusable environment-side structure rather than model-specific behavior. These results position runtime interface adaptation as a complementary alternative to model-centric agent training. Code is available at GitHub.

</details>


### [58] [Measuring Cross-Modal Synergy: A Benchmark for VLM Explainability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22168)
*Joël Roman Ky,Salah Ghamizi,Maxime Cordy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示了现有VLM可解释性评估范式的根本缺陷：由于跨模态冗余现象，单模态扰动指标会导致视觉和文本排名相互矛盾（评估崩塌）。为此提出协同保真度指标，基于Shapley交互指数严格隔离模态间的联合贡献，在保持高准确性的同时实现24倍计算加速。研究发现现有VLM解释器过度依赖视觉显著性，而改进的注意力方法在捕捉真实跨模态协同方面表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前视觉-语言模型（VLM）的可解释性评估依赖事后解释器和单模态扰动指标，但这种范式存在关键缺陷：由于多模态数据集包含语言先验和模态偏差，VLM经常表现出跨模态冗余，仅使用文本就能回答视觉查询。这导致单模态指标错误惩罚忠实的解释器，引发评估崩塌，使视觉和文本排名根本性矛盾（Kendall's τ= -0.06）。需要新的评估框架来准确度量跨模态推理过程。

Method: 提出协同保真度（Synergistic Faithfulness, $\mathcal{F}_{syn}$）指标，该方法基于Shapley交互指数（Shapley Interaction Index），严格隔离模态间的联合Harsanyi红利（joint Harsanyi dividend）。该方法作为高度准确的代理指标（ρ= 0.92），同时实现24倍计算加速。该方法解耦了视觉合理性和跨模态保真度，能够在3个VLM架构和3个基准数据集上系统评估8种不同的XAI方法。

Result: 实验结果表明：1）协同保真度指标与真实跨模态协同高度相关（ρ= 0.92），计算效率提升24倍；2）为VLM提出的解释器过度依赖视觉显著性，在捕捉真实跨模态协同方面显著表现不佳；3）改进的基于注意力的方法在捕获跨模态协同方面优于现有VLM专用解释器；4）现有评估框架导致视觉和文本排名根本性矛盾（Kendall's τ= -0.06）。

Conclusion: 本研究通过解耦视觉合理性与跨模态保真度，为VLM推理提供了严格的评估框架。该方法解决了现有评估范式的根本性问题，为高风险部署环境中安全审计VLM推理提供了必要工具。研究揭示了现有VLM解释方法的局限性，为未来跨模态可解释性研究指明了方向。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) map complex visual inputs to semantic spaces, but interpreting the cross-modal reasoning of VLMs currently relies on post-hoc explainers evaluated via unimodal perturbation metrics. We expose a limitation in this paradigm: because multimodal datasets contain language priors and modality biases, VLMs frequently exhibit cross-modal redundancy, allowing them to answer visual queries using text alone. Consequently, unimodal metrics penalize faithful explainers, triggering an evaluation collapse where visual and textual rankings fundamentally contradict each other. %(Kendall's $τ= -0.06$). To resolve this, we introduce Synergistic Faithfulness ($\mathcal{F}_{syn}$), a scalable metric rooted in the Shapley Interaction Index that strictly isolates the joint Harsanyi dividend between modalities, serving as a highly accurate surrogate ($ρ= 0.92$) while achieving a $24\times$ computational speedup. Evaluating 8 distinct XAI methods across 3 VLM architectures and 3 benchmark datasets, reveals that explainers proposed for VLMs heavily over-index on visual salience and significantly underperform adapted attention-based methods in capturing true cross-modal synergy. By decoupling visual plausibility from cross-modal faithfulness, this work provides a rigorous evaluation framework required to safely audit VLM reasoning in high-stakes deployments.

</details>


### [59] [LLM-Metrics: Measuring Research Impact Through Large Language Model Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22176)
*Si Shen,Wenhua Zhao,Danhao Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出LLM-Metrics，利用大语言模型的参数记忆来评估论文影响力。通过549篇计算机科学论文和17个LLM的测试，发现15个模型产生正向预测，整体与引用量的斯皮尔曼相关系数为0.1495，为实时、跨学科、独立于引用的研究评估提供了新范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的研究影响力评估主要依赖引用量，但存在明显缺陷：时间滞后性、学科偏见和马太效应。作者假设高影响力论文在学术界获得更多曝光，这些曝光以文本形式进入LLM训练数据，从而使模型形成更强的参数记忆。基于这一假设，作者旨在开发一种新的影响力评估指标。

Method: 设计了四种类型的多选题探测任务：标题识别、作者识别、方法识别和发表场所识别。在17个来自六个供应商的LLM上评估了549篇2023-2024年发表的计算机科学论文，模型参数规模从0.5B到72B不等。通过分析模型对这些论文的回答准确性，检验其与引用量的相关性。

Result: 17个模型中有15个产生正向预测，其中9个在p<0.05水平上显著，整体与引用量的斯皮尔曼相关系数ρ=0.1495(p=0.0004)。三项发现支持所提机制：1)对2024年论文的预测信号更强(ρ=0.1880)，而此时这些论文的引用量几乎为零，降低了反向因果解释的可能性；2)作者识别探测任务表现出最强的辨别力；3)模型规模与预测能力呈非单调关系：3B参数的Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct(ρ=0.1829)优于大多数更大的模型，支持选择性记忆假设——小模型的有限容量可作为有效的信息过滤器。

Conclusion: LLM-Metrics提供了一种实时、跨学科、不依赖引用的研究评估新范式，为传统引用指标提供了补充方案。研究揭示了LLM参数记忆与研究影响力之间的关联，并发现模型规模与预测能力之间的非单调关系，这对理解LLM的信息筛选机制具有启示意义。

Abstract: Citation counts remain the dominant metric for assessing research impact, yet they suffer from well-documented limitations: temporal lag, disciplinary bias, and Matthew effects. Here we propose LLM-Metrics, a research-impact assessment metric derived from the parametric memory of large language models (LLMs). The central hypothesis is that high-impact papers receive greater exposure in the academic community, that this exposure enters LLM training data in textual form, and that models consequently form stronger parametric memory of these papers. We designed four types of multiple-choice probes, covering title recognition, author recognition, method recognition, and venue recognition, and evaluated 549 computer science papers published in 2023-2024 across 17 LLMs spanning 0.5B to 72B parameters from six vendors. Of the 17 models, 15 produced positive predictions, 9 of which were significant at p less than 0.05, with an overall Spearman correlation of rho = 0.1495 and p = 0.0004 against citation counts. Three additional findings support the proposed mechanism. First, the predictive signal was stronger for 2024 papers, rho = 0.1880, whose citation counts were near zero at model-training time, reducing the plausibility of a simple reverse-causality explanation. Second, author-recognition probes showed the strongest discriminative power, consistent with an exposure-driven memory mechanism. Third, model scale and predictive power were non-monotonic: a 3B-parameter model, Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct, with rho = 0.1829, outperformed most larger models, supporting a selective-memory hypothesis in which the limited capacity of smaller models can serve as an effective information filter. LLM-Metrics offers a real-time, cross-disciplinary, citation-independent paradigm for research assessment.

</details>


### [60] [Skill Weaving: Efficient LLM Improvement via Modular Skillpacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22205)
*Zhuo Li,Guodong Du,Zesheng Shi,Weiyang Guo,Weijun Yao,Yuan Zhou,Jiabo Zhang,Jing Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了SkillWeave框架，通过将通用大模型的能力分割为轻量级领域特定模块(skillpacks)，在固定内存预算下实现大模型的多领域专业化，并实现高效压缩与低延迟推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型专业化方法难以在多领域能力与严格的内存和推理约束之间取得平衡，迫切需要一种在固定内存预算下实现多领域专业化的方案。

Method: 提出SkillWeave模块化改进框架：1) 将通用模型的全能力分割为轻量级领域特定增量模块(skillpacks)；2) 集成SkillZip压缩技术将skillpacks压缩为紧凑的推理就绪格式，支持低延迟执行下的强多领域性能。

Result: 在多任务和智能体基准测试中，9B参数的SkillWeave模型超越多个基线，甚至优于32B参数的整体式大模型，同时实现最高4倍加速。

Conclusion: SkillWeave成功解决了大模型专业化中的内存与推理约束问题，证明了通过模块化分解和压缩技术，较小规模的模型能够在多领域任务上超越大规模单体模型，为大模型部署提供了高效可行的方案。

Abstract: Large language models increasingly require specialization across diverse domains, yet existing approaches struggle to balance multi-domain capacities with strict memory and inference constraints. In this work, we introduce SkillWeave, a modular improvement framework that enables LLMs to specialize under fixed memory budgets. SkillWeave partitions full capabilities of a general-purpose model into skillpacks -- lightweight, domain-specific delta modules -- that reorganize and refine the model's internal knowledge. For efficient deployment, SkillWeave integrates SkillZip to compress skillpacks into compact and inference-ready format, enabling strong multi-domain performance with low-latency execution. On multi-task and agentic benchmarks, a 9B SkillWeave model outperforms several baselines and even surpasses a 32B monolithic LLM, while achieving up to 4x speedup.

</details>


### [61] [CLORE: Content-Level Optimization for Reasoning Efficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22211)
*Yuyang Wu,Qiyao Xue,Guanxing Lu,Weichen Liu,Zihan Wang,Manling Li,Olexandr Isayev*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CLORE框架，通过内容级优化（删除重复、不相关或冗余推理片段）来提升大模型推理效率，在保持准确率的同时显著缩短推理轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习后训练方法生成的推理轨迹往往过长、重复或语义不透明，而现有的高效推理方法主要通过显式预算或长度感知奖励来控制响应长度，对中间推理内容的监督较弱。

Method: CLORE使用外部增强模型删除正确的策略轨迹中的重复段落、不可读或任务无关内容以及确定解决方案后的多余推理，保留最终答案。生成的增强-原始对通过辅助的无参考DPO目标与标准策略梯度训练联合优化。限制增强仅在正确轨迹上进行并执行局部删除，保持编辑后的轨迹接近策略分布，缓解离策略不匹配问题。

Result: 在DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B和Qwen2.5-Math-7B上的五个数学推理基准测试表明，CLORE改善了准确率-效率权衡，并且与GRPO、DAPO、Training Efficient和ThinkPrune等现有方法兼容。内容级分析显示CLORE减少了重复推理、不可读内容和答案后探索。

Conclusion: 内容级监督是长度级控制的互补方向，CLORE证明了通过编辑正确轨迹内容可以有效提升推理效率，为高效推理研究提供了新思路。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning post-training has improved the reasoning ability of large language models, but often produces unnecessarily long, repetitive, or semantically opaque reasoning traces. Existing efficient reasoning methods mainly regulate response length through explicit budgets or length-aware rewards, leaving intermediate reasoning content weakly supervised. We propose CLORE, a content-level optimization framework that improves reasoning efficiency by editing correct on-policy rollouts. CLORE uses an external augmentation model to delete repetitive segments, illegible or task-irrelevant content, and superfluous reasoning after the solution is established, while preserving the final answer. The resulting augmented--original pairs are optimized with an auxiliary reference-free DPO objective alongside standard policy-gradient training. By restricting augmentation to correct trajectories and performing local deletion, CLORE keeps edited rollouts close to the policy distribution and mitigates off-policy mismatch. Experiments on DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B and Qwen2.5-Math-7B across five mathematical reasoning benchmarks show that CLORE improves the accuracy--efficiency trade-off and remains compatible with GRPO, DAPO, Training Efficient, and ThinkPrune. Content-level analyses further show that CLORE reduces repetitive reasoning, illegible content, and post-answer exploration, supporting content-level supervision as a complementary direction to length-level control.

</details>


### [62] [Towards a compositional semantics for quantitative confidence assessment in assurance arguments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22213)
*Benjamin Herd,Jessica Kelly,Jan Sabsch,Lydia Gauerhof*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于主观逻辑(SL)的置信度语义，将保证论证转化为可分析的网络，解决GSN等符号缺乏操作语义的问题，实现统一、可组合的置信度评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的保证论证符号（如GSN）缺乏用于推导置信度的操作语义，现有方法主要在真值上推理而非论证主张的置信度；而现有的基于主观逻辑的方法无法提供覆盖所有论证元素和关系的统一、可组合语义。

Method: 提出一种置信度语义，将论证元素表示为主观逻辑观点，将元素间的关系映射到主观逻辑算子以模拟置信度的流动，从而将论证转化为可分析的置信度网络。

Result: 该方法提供了明确的论证依据、对上下文的原则性处理、溯源能力的保留、与GSN的兼容性，并通过示例性保证置信度评估提供了实用指导。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于主观逻辑的置信度语义方法为保证论证提供了统一且可操作的置信度评估框架，有效解决了现有方法的不足。

Abstract: Assurance arguments provide a clear and structured way to explain why stakeholders should trust that a system satisfies certain properties, yet widely used notations, e.g.Goal Structuring Notation (GSN), typically lack an operational semantics for deriving assurance confidence. Existing approaches address structure and soundness but largely reason over truth values, not over confidence in the justification of claims. Subjective Logic (SL) offers a calculus of belief, disbelief, and uncertainty with operators for combining opinions, enabling confidence propagation under incomplete, conflicting, or subjective evidence. However, existing SL-based approaches do not provide a uniform, compositional semantics that covers all argument elements and relations to enable overall confidence assessment. We propose a confidence semantics that represents argument elements as SL opinions and maps relations between elements to SL operators modelling how confidence flows, effectively turning the argument into an analyzable confidence network. The approach provides explicit warrants, principled handling of context, preserved provenance, and compatibility with GSN, along with practical guidance using an exemplary assurance confidence assessment.

</details>


### [63] [SGR-Bench: Benchmarking Search Agents on State-Gated Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22219)
*Ningyuan Li,Haiyang Shen,Mugeng Liu,Yudong Han,Zhuofan Shi,Sixiong Xie,Yun Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了SGR-Bench基准，用于评估“状态门控检索”（SGR）能力，即需要在专业数据检索网站上通过设置过滤器、视图等建立正确的站点特定检索状态才能获取答案的任务。评估显示现有系统表现不佳，主要失败原因是检索状态配置错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型和工具使用智能体的基准测试尚未充分覆盖一类重要的专业检索任务。在许多专业数据检索网站上，包含答案的证据只有在通过过滤器、视图、层级或范围建立正确的站点特定检索状态后才可访问。这种能力被称为状态门控检索（SGR），目前缺乏系统性的评估基准。

Method: 作者构建了SGR-Bench基准数据集，包含100个专家策划的任务，涵盖6个来源家族和12个公共数据生态系统。每个任务要求智能体发现合适的网站并配置其站点特定的检索状态以生成结构化答案。基准提供了约束引导和目标导向两种问题表述，以支持显式和隐式引导的对比分析。作者评估了8个CLI智能体LLM系统和3个商用搜索智能体产品。

Result: 表现最好的系统在SGR-Bench上仅达到66.18%的项目级F1分数，行级F1更低。对156个可分析的失败轨迹的人工审计表明：智能体经常能到达相关网页源，但建立了错误的站点特定检索状态。检索范围漂移（37.2%）和标准不匹配（27.6%）是主要失败原因，而最终答案组合仅占10.3%。

Conclusion: 状态门控检索是当前智能体系统的薄弱环节。主要挑战不在于定位信息源或答案组合，而在于正确理解和配置网站特定的检索状态（如过滤条件、视图设置等）。SGR-Bench为这一能力的评估和改进提供了标准化的测试平台。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models and tool-using agents have expanded the range of benchmarked web tasks. Yet an important class of specialized retrieval tasks remains undercharacterized. On many specialized data-retrieval websites, answer-bearing evidence becomes accessible only after establishing the correct site-specific retrieval state through filters, views, hierarchies, or scopes. We term this capability state-gated retrieval (SGR). We introduce SGR-Bench, a benchmark for this setting containing 100 expert-curated tasks spanning six source families and 12 public data ecosystems. Each task requires discovering the appropriate website and configuring its site-specific retrieval state to produce a structured answer. SGR-Bench pairs constraint-guided and goal-oriented formulations of the same underlying problems, enabling controlled comparisons between explicit and implicit guidance for state-gated retrieval. We evaluate eight CLI-based agentic LLM systems and three commercial search-agent products. On SGR-Bench, the strongest system reaches only 66.18% item-level F1, while row-level F1 remains much lower. A manual audit of 156 analyzable failed CLI trajectories shows why: agents often reach a relevant web source, but establish the wrong site-specific retrieval state. Retrieval-scope drift (37.2%) and criterion mismatch (27.6%) dominate, whereas final answer composition accounts for only 10.3%. The dataset and single-case evaluation instructions are available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/PKUAIWeb/SGR-BENCH.

</details>


### [64] [Evaluating Large Language Models as Live Strategic Agents: Provider Performance, Hybrid Decomposition, and Operational Gaps in Timed Risk Play](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22238)
*H. C. Ekne*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究LLM在真实循环系统环境中的表现。Gemini在32场锦标赛中获胜20场，显著优于其他模型；但当分离规划与执行环节后，各模型规划能力差异不显著。分析发现Gemini更频繁追踪目标并形成更长的征服链，证明了在线智能体性能取决于目标追踪、执行转化、成本和运行时可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的静态基准测试仅能部分反映大语言模型在实际应用中的行为表现。真实系统将模型置于具有时间限制、格式约束和失败模式的重复循环中，这种动态环境下的模型表现尚未得到充分研究。因此需要评估LLM作为有界工作流组件而非孤立基准测试响应者的性能。

Method: 构建具有明确胜利目标和重复规划执行循环的定时多阶段风险环境；进行32场跨提供商锦标赛复现实验；通过标准化执行层（使用Gemini Flash脚手架）分离规划与执行环节；分析保存的规划和执行轨迹，研究模型行为机制。

Result: 在冻结规则下的32场锦标赛中，gemini-3.1-pro-preview赢得20场，胜率分布显著异于等强度零假设（p≈1.5×10^-5）；分离规划与执行后，32场规划对比实验显示各模型接近等强（p≈0.821），表明早期差异主要来自端到端系统行为而非单纯规划能力；Gemini更频繁地引用终端目标（随胜利临近而增加），并转化更多回合为深度征服链。

Conclusion: 在线智能体性能依赖于目标追踪、执行转化、成本和运行时可靠性等多个因素；研究结果支持将LLM作为有界工作流组件进行评估，而非仅作为孤立的基准测试响应者；这为LLM在复杂动态环境中的实际部署提供了更全面的评估视角。

Abstract: Static benchmarks capture only part of how large language models behave in practice. Real systems place models inside repeated loops with time limits, formatting constraints, and failure modes. We study this setting in a timed multi-phase Risk environment with explicit victory targets and repeated planning and execution cycles. In a replicated 32-game cross-provider championship under frozen rules, gemini-3.1-pro-preview won 20 of 32 games against gpt-5.1, claude-opus-4-7, and kimi-k2.6, and the pooled winner distribution differs strongly from an equal-strength null (p approx 1.5 x 10^-5). We then separate planning from execution by standardizing execution on a cheaper Gemini Flash scaffold. Under this design, a pooled 32-game planner bakeoff is consistent with near-equality (p approx 0.821), which indicates that much of the earlier provider spread came from end-to-end system behavior rather than planning alone. To study mechanism, we analyze saved planning and execution traces from the provider championship. Gemini refers to the terminal objective far more often than the other models and increases that focus as victory approaches. Gemini also converts more turns into deep conquest chains, even though it is not the cleanest runtime.
